`simulator/bootrom.py`:

```py
"""Serve the simulated Dot's bootrom, then amonet's payload, on a pseudo-terminal.

python3 simulator/bootrom.py WORK
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import collections
import enum
import hashlib
import os
import signal
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import tty
import zipfile

import dot

ACKNOWLEDGEMENT = b"\xd0\xd0\xd0\xd0"
AES_LENGTH = 1
AES_SLOT_POINTERS = [18, 26, 26]
AREAS = {0: "user", 1: "boot0", 2: "boot1"}
BLOCK = 512
BOOTROM_COMMANDS = frozenset({0xC8, 0xD1, 0xD4})
DECRYPT_FUNCTION = 126
EMMC_HEADER = struct.Struct(">cBQI")
EXTENDED = 0xC8
FAST_SEND_PATTERN = bytes((index * 7) & 0xFF for index in range(0x200))
FINISHED = 0x1
FLUSH_ADDRESS = 0x201000
FLUSH_LENGTH = 4
GCPU = 0x10210000
GCPU_ACQUIRE = (0x1F, 0x12000)
GCPU_COMMAND = GCPU + 0x0C00
GCPU_DESTINATION = GCPU + 0x0C08
GCPU_INPUT = GCPU + 0x0C68
GCPU_INTERRUPT_CLEAR = GCPU + 0x0804
GCPU_INTERRUPT_STATUS = GCPU + 0x0800
GCPU_LENGTH = GCPU + 0x0C0C
GCPU_MONITOR = GCPU + 0x0418
GCPU_PROGRAM_CONTROL = GCPU + 0x0400
GCPU_SLOT_POINTERS = GCPU + 0x0C14
HANDSHAKE = ((0xA0, 0x5F), (0x0A, 0xF5), (0x50, 0xAF), (0x05, 0xFA))
JUMP_REGISTER = 0x1028A8
MAGIC = 0xF00DD00D
MAXIMUM_BLOCKS = 64
MMIO_START = 0x10000000
PAYLOAD_ADDRESS = 0x201000
PAYLOAD_VERSIONS = ("v1.1.0", "v2.0.0")
RANGE_CHECK = 0x102868
RANGE_CHECK_OFF = bytes(12) + b"\x80" + bytes(3)
READY = b"\xb1\xb2\xb3\xb4"
READ_STATUS = b"\x00\x00"
READ_WORDS = 0xD1
REFUSED = b"\x1d\x0c"
REFUSED_STATUS = 0x3
RPMB_AREA = 3
RPMB_SIZE = 0x100
SHOWN_WORDS = 16
START_WAIT = 10
V1_COMMANDS = frozenset({0x1000, 0x1001, 0x1002, 0x2000, 0x2001, 0x3000})
WRITE_STATUS = b"\x00\x01"
WRITE_WORDS = 0xD4
ZERO_DECRYPTION = bytes.fromhex("4dd12bdf0ec7d26c482490b3482a1b1f")


class Bootrom:
    def __init__(self, *, payloads: dict[str, bytes], port: Port) -> None:
        self.acquired = False
        self.defeated = False
        self.memory: dict[int, int] = {}
        self.payloads = payloads
        self.port = port
        self.recorder = Recorder()

    def aes(self) -> int:
        pointers = [
            self.memory.get(GCPU_SLOT_POINTERS + 4 * index, 0) for index in range(3)
        ]
        if (
            not self.acquired
            or self.memory.get(GCPU_COMMAND) != DECRYPT_FUNCTION
            or self.memory.get(GCPU_LENGTH) != AES_LENGTH
            or pointers != AES_SLOT_POINTERS
        ):
            return REFUSED_STATUS
        mask = struct.unpack("<4I", ZERO_DECRYPTION)
        address = self.memory.get(GCPU_DESTINATION, 0)
        for index in range(4):
            value = self.memory.get(GCPU_INPUT + 4 * index, 0) ^ mask[index]
            self.memory[address + 4 * index] = value
        found = self.bytes_at(address=RANGE_CHECK, length=len(RANGE_CHECK_OFF))
        self.defeated = self.defeated or found == RANGE_CHECK_OFF
        return FINISHED

    def allowed(self, *, address: int) -> bool:
        return self.defeated or address >= MMIO_START

    def bytes_at(self, *, address: int, length: int) -> bytes:
        return b"".join(
            struct.pack("<I", self.memory.get(address + offset, 0))
            for offset in range(0, length, 4)
        )[:length]

    def echo(self, *, length: int) -> int:
        data = self.port.read(length=length)
        self.port.write(data=data)
        return int.from_bytes(data, "big")

    def handshake(self) -> None:
        step = 0
        while step < len(HANDSHAKE):
            sent, answer = HANDSHAKE[step]
            if self.port.read(length=1)[0] == sent:
                self.port.write(data=bytes([answer]))
                step += 1
            else:
                step = 0
        self.recorder.say(tool="bootrom", words=["handshake"])

    def identify(self) -> str | None:
        for name, payload in self.payloads.items():
            padded = payload + bytes(-len(payload) % 4)
            if self.bytes_at(address=PAYLOAD_ADDRESS, length=len(padded)) == padded:
                return name
        return None

    def jump(self, *, target: int) -> str:
        found = self.identify() if target == PAYLOAD_ADDRESS else None
        self.recorder.say(
            tool="bootrom", words=["jump", f"{target:#x}", found or "unknown code"]
        )
        while found is None:
            self.port.read(length=1)
        self.port.write(data=READY)
        return found

    def read_words(self) -> None:
        address = self.echo(length=4)
        count = self.echo(length=4)
        if not self.allowed(address=address):
            self.recorder.say(
                tool="bootrom", words=["read32", f"{address:#x}", f"refused {count}"]
            )
            self.port.write(data=REFUSED)
            return
        self.recorder.say(tool="bootrom", words=["read32", f"{address:#x}", str(count)])
        values = [self.memory.get(address + 4 * index, 0) for index in range(count)]
        self.port.write(
            data=READ_STATUS + struct.pack(f">{count}I", *values) + READ_STATUS
        )

    def serve(self) -> str:
        self.handshake()
        while True:
            command = self.port.read(length=1)[0]
            if command not in BOOTROM_COMMANDS:
                self.recorder.say(tool="bootrom", words=["unknown", f"{command:#04x}"])
                continue
            self.port.write(data=bytes([command]))
            if command == READ_WORDS:
                self.read_words()
            elif command == WRITE_WORDS:
                jumped = self.write_words()
                if jumped is not None:
                    return jumped
            else:
                subcommand = self.echo(length=1)
                self.port.write(data=bytes(3))
                self.recorder.say(tool="bootrom", words=["ext", f"{subcommand:#04x}"])

    def store(self, *, address: int, word: int) -> None:
        self.memory[address] = word
        if address == GCPU:
            self.acquired = False
        if address == GCPU + 4:
            self.acquired = (self.memory.get(GCPU), word) == GCPU_ACQUIRE
        if address == GCPU_INTERRUPT_CLEAR:
            self.memory[GCPU_INTERRUPT_STATUS] = 0
            self.memory[GCPU_MONITOR] = 0
        if address == GCPU_PROGRAM_CONTROL:
            status = self.aes()
            self.memory[GCPU_INTERRUPT_STATUS] = status
            self.memory[GCPU_MONITOR] = status & FINISHED

    def write_words(self) -> str | None:
        address = self.echo(length=4)
        count = self.echo(length=4)
        if not self.allowed(address=address):
            self.recorder.say(
                tool="bootrom", words=["write32", f"{address:#x}", f"refused {count}"]
            )
            self.port.write(data=REFUSED)
            return None
        self.port.write(data=WRITE_STATUS)
        words = [self.echo(length=4) for _ in range(count)]
        if count > SHOWN_WORDS:
            data = struct.pack(f"<{count}I", *words)
            shown = [f"{count} words", dot.md5(data=data)]
        else:
            shown = [f"{word:#x}" for word in words]
        self.recorder.say(tool="bootrom", words=["write32", f"{address:#x}", *shown])
        for index, word in enumerate(words):
            self.store(address=address + 4 * index, word=word)
        if address == JUMP_REGISTER and count == 1:
            return self.jump(target=words[0])
        self.port.write(data=WRITE_STATUS)
        return None


class Command(enum.IntEnum):
    READ_BLOCK = 0x1000
    WRITE_BLOCK = 0x1001
    SWITCH_PARTITION = 0x1002
    WRITE_BLOCKS = 0x1003
    READ_BLOCKS = 0x1004
    READ_RPMB = 0x2000
    WRITE_RPMB = 0x2001
    REBOOT = 0x3000
    KICK_WATCHDOG = 0x3001
    READ_MEMORY = 0x5000
    TRY_FAST_SEND = 0x5002
    STOCK_SEND = 0x5003


class Emmc:
    def __init__(self, *, container: str) -> None:
        self.process = subprocess.Popen(
            ["docker", "exec", "-i", container, "/sim/bin/sim-emmc"],
            bufsize=0,
            stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
        )

    def answer(self, *, length: int) -> bytes:
        data = b""
        while len(data) < length:
            piece = self.process.stdout.read(length - len(data))
            if not piece:
                message = "the eMMC helper stopped"
                raise OSError(message)
            data += piece
        return data

    def read(self, *, area: int, count: int, first: int) -> bytes | None:
        with dot.busy():
            self.process.stdin.write(EMMC_HEADER.pack(b"r", area, first, count))
            status = self.answer(length=1)
            data = self.answer(length=count * BLOCK)
        return data if status == b"k" else None

    def write(self, *, area: int, data: bytes, first: int) -> bool:
        header = EMMC_HEADER.pack(b"w", area, first, len(data) // BLOCK)
        with dot.busy():
            self.process.stdin.write(header + data)
            return self.answer(length=1) == b"k"


class Payload:
    def __init__(self, *, bootrom: Bootrom, emmc: Emmc, version: str) -> None:
        self.area = 0
        self.bootrom = bootrom
        self.commands = V1_COMMANDS if version == "v1.1.0" else frozenset(Command)
        self.emmc = emmc
        self.port = bootrom.port
        self.recorder = bootrom.recorder

    def area_name(self) -> str:
        return AREAS.get(self.area, f"area {self.area}")

    def check_count(self, *, command: int, count: int, first: int) -> None:
        if (
            command in {Command.READ_BLOCKS, Command.WRITE_BLOCKS}
            and not 1 <= count <= MAXIMUM_BLOCKS
        ):
            self.halt(words=[f"{command:#06x}", "count", f"{first}+{count}"])

    def halt(self, *, words: list[str]) -> None:
        self.recorder.say(tool="payload", words=["halted", *words])
        while True:
            self.port.read(length=1)

    def read(self, *, command: int, count: int, first: int) -> None:
        data = (
            self.emmc.read(area=self.area, count=count, first=first)
            if self.area in AREAS
            else None
        )
        if data is None:
            self.halt(words=["read failed", self.area_name(), f"{first}+{count}"])
        self.recorder.access(
            area=self.area_name(),
            command=command,
            data=data,
            first=first,
            operation="read",
        )
        self.port.write(data=data)

    def read_memory(self, *, command: int) -> None:
        address, length = self.port.word(), self.port.word()
        if address == FLUSH_ADDRESS and length == FLUSH_LENGTH:
            self.recorder.count(command=command)
        else:
            self.recorder.say(
                tool="payload", words=["read memory", f"{address:#x}", str(length)]
            )
        self.port.write(
            data=self.bootrom.bytes_at(address=address, length=(length + 3) & ~3)
        )

    def rpmb(self, *, data: bytes | None) -> bytes:
        block = self.emmc.read(area=RPMB_AREA, count=1, first=0) or bytes(BLOCK)
        if data is not None:
            block = data + block[RPMB_SIZE:]
            self.emmc.write(area=RPMB_AREA, data=block, first=0)
        return block[:RPMB_SIZE]

    def serve(self) -> None:
        while True:
            if self.port.word() != MAGIC:
                self.recorder.say(tool="payload", words=["no magic"])
                continue
            command = self.port.word()
            if command not in self.commands:
                self.recorder.say(tool="payload", words=["unknown", f"{command:#06x}"])
                continue
            if command == Command.REBOOT:
                self.recorder.say(tool="payload", words=["reboot"])
                return
            self.step(command=command)

    def step(self, *, command: int) -> None:  # ruff: ignore[complex-structure]
        if command == Command.READ_BLOCK:
            self.read(command=command, count=1, first=self.port.word())
        elif command == Command.READ_BLOCKS:
            first, count = self.port.word(), self.port.word()
            self.check_count(command=command, count=count, first=first)
            self.read(command=command, count=count, first=first)
        elif command in {Command.WRITE_BLOCK, Command.WRITE_BLOCKS}:
            first = self.port.word()
            count = 1 if command == Command.WRITE_BLOCK else self.port.word()
            self.check_count(command=command, count=count, first=first)
            data = self.port.read(length=count * BLOCK)
            self.write(command=command, data=data, first=first)
        elif command == Command.SWITCH_PARTITION:
            self.area = self.port.word()
            self.recorder.say(tool="payload", words=["switch", self.area_name()])
        elif command == Command.READ_RPMB:
            found = self.rpmb(data=None)
            self.recorder.say(tool="payload", words=["rpmb read", dot.md5(data=found)])
            self.port.write(data=found)
        elif command == Command.WRITE_RPMB:
            data = self.port.read(length=RPMB_SIZE)
            self.rpmb(data=data)
            self.recorder.say(tool="payload", words=["rpmb write", dot.md5(data=data)])
        elif command == Command.KICK_WATCHDOG:
            self.recorder.count(command=command)
        elif command == Command.READ_MEMORY:
            self.read_memory(command=command)
        elif command == Command.TRY_FAST_SEND:
            self.recorder.say(tool="payload", words=["fast send"])
            self.port.write(data=FAST_SEND_PATTERN)
        elif command == Command.STOCK_SEND:
            self.recorder.say(tool="payload", words=["stock send"])
            self.port.write(data=ACKNOWLEDGEMENT)

    def write(self, *, command: int, data: bytes, first: int) -> None:
        written = self.area in AREAS and self.emmc.write(
            area=self.area, data=data, first=first
        )
        if not written:
            self.halt(
                words=[
                    "write failed",
                    self.area_name(),
                    f"{first}+{len(data) // BLOCK}",
                ]
            )
        self.recorder.access(
            area=self.area_name(),
            command=command,
            data=data,
            first=first,
            operation="write",
        )
        self.port.write(data=ACKNOWLEDGEMENT)


class Port:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.master, self.slave = os.openpty()
        tty.setraw(self.slave)
        self.name = os.ttyname(self.slave)

    def read(self, *, length: int) -> bytes:
        data = b""
        while len(data) < length:
            data += os.read(self.master, length - len(data))
        return data

    def word(self) -> int:
        return struct.unpack(">I", self.read(length=4))[0]

    def write(self, *, data: bytes) -> None:
        view = memoryview(data)
        while view:
            view = view[os.write(self.master, view) :]


class Recorder:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.loose: collections.Counter[str] = collections.Counter()
        self.run: Run | None = None

    def access(
        self, *, area: str, command: int, data: bytes, first: int, operation: str
    ) -> None:
        current = self.run
        if (
            current is None
            or current.operation != operation
            or current.area != area
            or current.last + 1 != first
        ):
            self.flush()
            current = self.run = Run(area=area, first=first, operation=operation)
        current.commands[f"{command:#06x}"] += 1
        current.digest.update(data)
        current.last = first + len(data) // BLOCK - 1

    def count(self, *, command: int) -> None:
        commands = self.loose if self.run is None else self.run.commands
        commands[f"{command:#06x}"] += 1

    def flush(self) -> None:
        current, self.run = self.run, None
        if self.loose:
            dot.record(
                entry={
                    "argv": ["between ranges"],
                    "commands": dict(sorted(self.loose.items())),
                    "loose": True,
                    "tool": "payload",
                }
            )
            self.loose.clear()
        if current is None:
            return
        dot.record(
            entry={
                "argv": [
                    current.operation,
                    current.area,
                    f"{current.first}+{current.last - current.first + 1}",
                    current.digest.hexdigest(),
                ],
                "commands": dict(sorted(current.commands.items())),
                "tool": "payload",
            }
        )

    def say(self, *, tool: str, words: list[str]) -> None:
        self.flush()
        dot.record(entry={"argv": words, "tool": tool})


class Run:
    def __init__(self, *, area: str, first: int, operation: str) -> None:
        self.area = area
        self.commands: collections.Counter[str] = collections.Counter()
        self.digest = hashlib.md5(usedforsecurity=False)
        self.first = first
        self.last = first - 1
        self.operation = operation


def claim(*, port: Port) -> dict | None:
    deadline = time.monotonic() + START_WAIT
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        with dot.locked():
            state = dot.load() if dot.STATE.exists() else {}
            if state.get("mode") == "bootrom":
                state.update(bootrom_pid=os.getpid(), port=port.name)
                dot.save(state=state)
                return state
        time.sleep(0.1)
    return None


def known_payloads(*, cache: str) -> dict[str, bytes]:
    found = {}
    for version in PAYLOAD_VERSIONS:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(f"{cache}/amonet-biscuit-{version}.zip") as archive:
            found[version] = dot.member(
                archive=archive, suffix="brom-payload/build/payload.bin"
            )
    return found


def main() -> int:
    if sys.argv[1:] != [str(dot.WORK)]:
        print(f"usage: {sys.argv[0]} {dot.WORK}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, stop)
    port = Port()
    state = claim(port=port)
    if state is None:
        return 1
    rebooted = False
    try:
        bootrom = Bootrom(payloads=known_payloads(cache=state["cache"]), port=port)
        try:
            version = bootrom.serve()
            bootrom.recorder.say(tool="payload", words=["ready", version])
            Payload(
                bootrom=bootrom,
                emmc=Emmc(container=state["container"]),
                version=version,
            ).serve()
            rebooted = True
        finally:
            bootrom.recorder.flush()
    finally:
        release(rebooted=rebooted)
    return 0


def release(*, rebooted: bool) -> None:
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIG_IGN)
    with dot.locked():
        if not dot.STATE.exists():
            return
        state = dot.load()
        if state.get("bootrom_pid") != os.getpid():
            return
        state.pop("bootrom_pid", None)
        state.pop("port", None)
        if rebooted:
            dot.reboot(into="system", state=state)
        dot.save(state=state)


def stop(*_: object) -> None:
    raise SystemExit(1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/python3
import pathlib
import sys

PROPS = pathlib.Path("/sim/run/props")
WITH_DEFAULT = 3

props = {}
if PROPS.exists():
    for line in PROPS.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        key, _, value = line.partition("=")
        props[key] = value
if len(sys.argv) > 1:
    print(props.get(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) >= WITH_DEFAULT else ""))
else:
    for key in sorted(props):
        print(f"[{key}]: [{props[key]}]")

`simulator/device/setprop`:

```
#!/usr/bin/python3
import os
import pathlib
import sys

PROPS = pathlib.Path("/sim/run/props")
REBOOT_PROPERTY = "sys.powerctl"
REQUEST = pathlib.Path("/sim/run/request")
SHELL_PROPERTIES = (
    "debug.",
    "log.",
    "service.adb.root",
    "service.adb.tcp.port",
    REBOOT_PROPERTY,
)
SHELL_UID = 2000
WITH_VALUE = 3

if len(sys.argv) < WITH_VALUE:
    print("usage: setprop <key> <value>", file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(1)
key, value = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
props = {}
if PROPS.exists():
    for line in PROPS.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        name, _, said = line.partition("=")
        props[name] = said
allowed = os.getuid() != SHELL_UID or key.startswith(SHELL_PROPERTIES)
if not allowed or (key.startswith("ro.") and key in props):
    sys.exit(0)
if key == REBOOT_PROPERTY:
    verb, _, into = value.partition(",")
    if verb == "reboot":
        REQUEST.write_text((into or "system") + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    sys.exit(0)
props[key] = value
PROPS.write_text(
    "".join(f"{name}={said}\n" for name, said in props.items()), encoding="utf-8"
)

```

`simulator/device/sim-boot`:

```
#!/usr/bin/python3
from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import fcntl
import hashlib
import json
import os
import pathlib
import shutil
import signal
import stat
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import time

BOOT_LAYOUT = b"BRLYT"
BY_NAME = pathlib.Path("/dev/block/platform/mtk-msdc.0/by-name")
DECOYS = {
    "lk_a": 1048576,
    "lk_b": 1048576,
    "preloader": 4194304,
    "tee": 5242880,
    "tee1": 5242880,
    "tee2": 5242880,
}
DECOY_ROOM = "64m"
DEVICES = pathlib.Path("/dev")
DISK = pathlib.Path("/sim/disk")
EMMC_BOOT = b"EMMC_BOOT"
FLAVOURS = pathlib.Path("/sim/flavours")
FOLDERS = {
    pathlib.Path("/data/local/tmp"): (2000, 0o771),
    pathlib.Path("/sdcard"): (2000, 0o777),
}
HOLDER_SECONDS = 5
LEFTOVER_SECONDS = 5
LOOP_CONTROL = "/dev/loop-control"
LOOP_CTL_GET_FREE = 0x4C82  # _IO('L', 0x82)
LOOP_MAJOR = 7
LOOP_TRIES = 50
MOUNT = pathlib.Path("/sim/mnt")
NULLED = ("lk_a", "lk_b", "tee1", "tee2")
PATH = pathlib.Path("/sim/path")
PROTECTED = ("boot0", "boot1")
RUN = pathlib.Path("/sim/run")
HOLDER = RUN / "holder"
HOLDER_LOG = RUN / "holder.log"
HOLDER_READY = RUN / "holder.ready"
LOOPS = RUN / "loops"
REQUEST = RUN / "request"
SECTOR = 512
SIDE_DEVICES = ("boot0", "boot1", "rpmb")
SIM_BOOT = ["/usr/bin/python3", "/sim/bin/sim-boot"]
SLOTS = ("a", "b")
SYSFS = "/sys/block"
SYSFS_LOG = RUN / "sysfs.log"
SYSFS_SECONDS = 5
TEMPORARY = pathlib.Path("/tmp")  # ruff: ignore[hardcoded-temp-file]
DECOY_FOLDER = TEMPORARY / "ota-decoy"


def act() -> None:
    action = sys.argv[1]
    if action == "boot":
        boot()
    elif action == "detach":
        detach()
    elif action == "enter":
        enter(spec=json.loads(sys.argv[2]))
    elif action == "facts":
        holding()
        print(json.dumps(facts(spec=json.loads(sys.argv[2]))))
    elif action == "snapshot":
        holding()
        snapshot()


def attached(*, device: str) -> list[str]:
    return subprocess.run(
        ["losetup", "-n", "-O", "BACK-INO,OFFSET,SIZELIMIT", "-l", device],
        capture_output=True,
        check=False,
        text=True,
    ).stdout.split()


def boot() -> None:
    kill_leftovers()
    unmount_all()
    detach()
    remove_block_nodes()
    BY_NAME.mkdir(parents=True)
    partitions = ["major minor  #blocks  name", ""]
    disk = DISK / "mmcblk0.img"
    node(minor=loop(path=disk), name="mmcblk0")
    partitions.append(f" 179        0 {disk.stat().st_size // 1024:>10} mmcblk0")
    starts = {}
    for number, name, first, sectors in table():
        node(
            minor=loop(offset=first * SECTOR, path=disk, size=sectors * SECTOR),
            name=f"mmcblk0p{number}",
        )
        (BY_NAME / name).symlink_to(f"/dev/block/mmcblk0p{number}")
        partitions.append(
            f" 179 {number:>8} {sectors * SECTOR // 1024:>10} mmcblk0p{number}"
        )
        starts[number] = (first, sectors)
    for minor, side in zip((96, 64, 32), ("rpmb", "boot1", "boot0")):
        path = DISK / f"{side}.img"
        node(minor=loop(path=path, protected=side in PROTECTED), name=f"mmcblk0{side}")
        partitions.append(
            f" 179 {minor:>8} {path.stat().st_size // 1024:>10} mmcblk0{side}"
        )
    hold()
    listing = RUN / "partitions"
    listing.write_text("\n".join(partitions) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    call(arguments=["mount", "--bind", str(listing), "/proc/partitions"])
    mount_sysfs()
    call(arguments=["mount", "-t", "tmpfs", "tmpfs", "/sys/class/block"])
    for number, (first, sectors) in starts.items():
        folder = pathlib.Path(f"/sys/class/block/mmcblk0p{number}")
        folder.mkdir()
        (folder / "start").write_text(f"{first}\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (folder / "size").write_text(f"{sectors}\n", encoding="utf-8")
    for leftover in TEMPORARY.iterdir():
        if leftover.is_dir() and not leftover.is_symlink():
            shutil.rmtree(leftover, ignore_errors=True)
        else:
            leftover.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    REQUEST.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    REQUEST.chmod(0o666)


def call(*, arguments: list[str]) -> str:
    return subprocess.run(
        arguments, capture_output=True, check=True, text=True
    ).stdout.strip()


def command_line(*, pid: int) -> list[str]:
    try:
        raw = pathlib.Path(f"/proc/{pid}/cmdline").read_bytes()
    except OSError:
        return []
    return raw.decode(errors="replace").split("\0")[:-1]


def detach() -> None:
    records = (
        [line.split() for line in LOOPS.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
        if LOOPS.exists()
        else []
    )
    for device, *_ in records:
        if not pathlib.Path(device).exists():
            number = int(device.removeprefix("/dev/loop"))
            os.mknod(device, 0o660 | stat.S_IFBLK, os.makedev(LOOP_MAJOR, number))
    ours = [
        (device, backing)
        for device, *backing in records
        if attached(device=device) == backing
    ]
    for device, _ in ours:
        quiet(arguments=["blockdev", "--setrw", device])
    if HOLDER.exists():
        pid = int(HOLDER.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if "/sim/bin/sim-hold" in command_line(pid=pid):
            with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                os.kill(pid, signal.SIGTERM)
            wait_gone(pid=pid, seconds=HOLDER_SECONDS)
        HOLDER.unlink()
    failed = [
        " ".join([device, *backing])
        for device, backing in ours
        if attached(device=device) == backing
        and subprocess.run(
            ["losetup", "-d", device], capture_output=True, check=False
        ).returncode
    ]
    LOOPS.write_text("".join(f"{line}\n" for line in failed), encoding="utf-8")
    if failed:
        message = f"could not detach {len(failed)} loops: {failed}"
        raise OSError(message)


def enter(*, spec: dict) -> None:
    props = RUN / "props"
    props.write_text(
        "".join(f"{key}={value}\n" for key, value in spec["props"].items()),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    props.chmod(0o666)
    shutil.rmtree(PATH, ignore_errors=True)
    shutil.copytree(FLAVOURS / spec["flavour"], PATH, symlinks=True)
    if spec["props"].get("sim.su") == "1":
        (PATH / "su").symlink_to("/opt/dot/su")
    for folder, (group, mode) in FOLDERS.items():
        folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
        os.chown(folder, 0, group if spec["uid"] else 0)
        folder.chmod(mode if spec["uid"] else 0o755)
    if spec.get("twrp") == "v1":
        null_bootloaders()
    elif spec.get("twrp") in {"bboe", "v2"}:
        if spec["twrp"] == "bboe":
            point_boot_at_fire_os()
        make_decoys()


def facts(*, spec: dict) -> dict:
    names = {name: number for number, name, _, _ in table()}
    result: dict = {"prefixes": {}, "table": sorted(names)}
    for name, sizes in spec["prefixes"].items():
        path = (
            pathlib.Path(f"/dev/block/mmcblk0{name}")
            if name in SIDE_DEVICES
            else BY_NAME / name
        )
        if path.exists():
            result["prefixes"][name] = {
                str(size): prefix(path=path, size=size) for size in sizes
            }
    with pathlib.Path("/dev/block/mmcblk0boot0").open("rb") as boot0:
        head = boot0.read(2 * SECTOR)
    result["preloader"] = head.startswith(EMMC_BOOT) and head[SECTOR:].startswith(
        BOOT_LAYOUT
    )
    for name in ("misc", "expdb"):
        path = BY_NAME / name
        if path.exists():
            with path.open("rb") as file:
                result[f"{name}_fastboot"] = file.read(15) == b"FASTBOOT_PLEASE"
    for slot in ("a", "b"):
        path = BY_NAME / f"system_{slot}"
        result[f"system_{slot}"] = (
            mounted_read(
                path=path,
                wanted={
                    "build": "build.prop",
                    "flags": "etc/init.fosflags.sh",
                },
            )
            if path.exists()
            else None
        )
        result[f"root_adb_{slot}"] = (RUN / f"root-adb-{slot}").exists()
    result["magisk"] = False
    MOUNT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    if not subprocess.run(
        ["mount", "-o", "ro", "-t", "ext4", str(BY_NAME / "userdata"), str(MOUNT)],
        capture_output=True,
        check=False,
    ).returncode:
        result["magisk"] = (MOUNT / "adb" / "magisk.db").exists()
        quiet(arguments=["umount", str(MOUNT)])
    result["active"] = (RUN / "active").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    return result


def hold() -> None:
    HOLDER_READY.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    devices = [
        line.split()[0] for line in LOOPS.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    ]
    with HOLDER_LOG.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as log:
        holder = subprocess.Popen(
            ["/sim/bin/sim-hold", str(HOLDER_READY), *devices],
            start_new_session=True,
            stderr=log,
            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stdout=log,
        )
    HOLDER.write_text(str(holder.pid), encoding="utf-8")
    deadline = time.monotonic() + HOLDER_SECONDS
    while not (
        HOLDER_READY.exists()
        and HOLDER_READY.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip() == str(len(devices))
    ):
        if holder.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() > deadline:
            message = (
                f"sim-hold did not hold {len(devices)} loops:"
                f" {log_tail(path=HOLDER_LOG)}"
            )
            raise OSError(message)
        time.sleep(0.05)


def holding() -> None:
    pid = int(HOLDER.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if HOLDER.exists() else 0
    state = ""
    with contextlib.suppress(OSError, IndexError):
        state = (
            pathlib
            .Path(f"/proc/{pid}/stat")
            .read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            .rsplit(")", 1)[1]
            .split()[0]
        )
    if (
        not pid
        or state in {"", "Z"}
        or "/sim/bin/sim-hold" not in command_line(pid=pid)
    ):
        message = f"sim-hold is not running: {log_tail(path=HOLDER_LOG)}"
        raise OSError(message)


def kill_leftovers() -> None:
    spared = {1, os.getpid()}
    children = [
        (status[19], pid) for pid, status in processes().items() if status[1] == "1"
    ]
    if children:
        spared.add(min(children, key=lambda child: int(child[0]))[1])
    holder = int(HOLDER.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if HOLDER.exists() else 0
    words = command_line(pid=holder)
    if "/sim/bin/sim-hold" in words and str(HOLDER_READY) in words:
        spared.add(holder)
    victims = []
    for pid in processes():
        words = command_line(pid=pid)
        if pid in spared or not words or words[: len(SIM_BOOT)] == SIM_BOOT:
            continue
        with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
            os.kill(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
            victims.append(pid)
    for pid in victims:
        wait_gone(pid=pid, seconds=LEFTOVER_SECONDS)


def link(*, path: pathlib.Path, target: pathlib.Path | str) -> None:
    path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    path.symlink_to(target)


def log_tail(*, path: pathlib.Path) -> str:
    with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
        return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")[-2000:].strip()
    return ""


def loop(
    *, offset: int = 0, path: pathlib.Path, protected: bool = False, size: int = 0
) -> int:
    arguments = ["losetup"]
    if offset:
        arguments += ["-o", str(offset)]
    if size:
        arguments += ["--sizelimit", str(size)]
    backing = [str(path.stat().st_ino), str(offset), str(size)]
    failures = []
    for _ in range(LOOP_TRIES):
        with pathlib.Path(LOOP_CONTROL).open("rb") as control:
            number = fcntl.ioctl(control, LOOP_CTL_GET_FREE)
        device = f"/dev/loop{number}"
        if not pathlib.Path(device).exists():
            os.mknod(device, 0o660 | stat.S_IFBLK, os.makedev(LOOP_MAJOR, number))
        tried = subprocess.run(
            [*arguments, device, str(path)], capture_output=True, check=False, text=True
        )
        if tried.returncode == 0:
            break
        failures.append(tried.stderr.strip())
    else:
        message = f"no loop device took {path}: {failures[-3:]}"
        raise OSError(message)
    found = attached(device=device)
    if found != backing:
        if found[:1] == backing[:1]:
            release(device=device)
        message = f"{device} is not {path} at {offset}+{size}: {found}"
        raise OSError(message)
    try:
        call(arguments=["blockdev", "--setro" if protected else "--setrw", device])
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
        if attached(device=device) == backing:
            release(device=device)
        raise
    with LOOPS.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as held:
        held.write(" ".join([device, *backing]) + "\n")
    return number


def main() -> None:
    RUN.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
    with (RUN / "lock").open("w") as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        act()


def make_decoys() -> None:
    DECOY_FOLDER.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    call(
        arguments=[
            "mount",
            "-t",
            "tmpfs",
            "-o",
            f"size={DECOY_ROOM}",
            "tmpfs",
            str(DECOY_FOLDER),
        ]
    )
    for part, size in DECOYS.items():
        named = BY_NAME / part
        if named.is_block_device():
            link(path=BY_NAME / f"{part}_real", target=named.resolve())
        decoy = DECOY_FOLDER / part
        with decoy.open("wb") as file:
            file.truncate(size)
        link(path=named, target=decoy)


def md5(*, data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.md5(data, usedforsecurity=False).hexdigest()


def mount_sysfs() -> None:
    call(arguments=["mount", "-t", "tmpfs", "-o", "size=1m", "tmpfs", SYSFS])
    with SYSFS_LOG.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as log:
        server = subprocess.Popen(
            [
                "/sim/bin/sim-sysfs",
                SYSFS,
                *(
                    str((DISK / f"{side}.img").stat().st_size // SECTOR)
                    for side in PROTECTED
                ),
            ],
            start_new_session=True,
            stderr=log,
            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stdout=log,
        )
    deadline = time.monotonic() + SYSFS_SECONDS
    while not (
        top_mount(target=SYSFS).startswith("fuse")
        and pathlib.Path(f"{SYSFS}/mmcblk0boot0/force_ro").exists()
    ):
        if server.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() > deadline:
            message = f"{SYSFS} did not mount: {log_tail(path=SYSFS_LOG)}"
            raise OSError(message)
        time.sleep(0.05)


def mounted_read(
    *, path: pathlib.Path, wanted: dict[str, str]
) -> dict[str, str] | None:
    MOUNT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    if subprocess.run(
        ["mount", "-o", "ro", "-t", "ext4", str(path), str(MOUNT)],
        capture_output=True,
        check=False,
    ).returncode:
        return None
    try:
        found = {}
        for key, relative in wanted.items():
            target = MOUNT / relative
            found[key] = target.read_text(errors="replace") if target.is_file() else ""
        return found
    finally:
        quiet(arguments=["umount", str(MOUNT)])


def node(*, minor: int, name: str) -> None:
    path = pathlib.Path("/dev/block") / name
    path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    os.mknod(path, 0o600 | stat.S_IFBLK, os.makedev(LOOP_MAJOR, minor))


def null_bootloaders() -> None:
    for slot in SLOTS:
        named = BY_NAME / f"boot_{slot}"
        amonet = named.resolve()
        link(path=named, target=(BY_NAME / f"boot_{slot}_x").resolve())
        link(path=BY_NAME / f"boot_{slot}_amonet", target=amonet)
    for part in NULLED:
        named = BY_NAME / part
        link(path=BY_NAME / f"{part}_real", target=named.resolve())
        link(path=named, target="/dev/null")


def point_boot_at_fire_os() -> None:
    for slot in SLOTS:
        named = BY_NAME / f"boot_{slot}"
        fire_os = BY_NAME / f"boot_{slot}_x"
        if (
            fire_os.is_block_device()
            and named.is_block_device()
            and fire_os.resolve() != named.resolve()
        ):
            amonet = named.resolve()
            link(path=named, target=fire_os.resolve())
            link(path=BY_NAME / f"boot_{slot}_amonet", target=amonet)


def prefix(*, path: pathlib.Path, size: int) -> str:
    with path.open("rb") as file:
        return md5(data=file.read(size))


def processes() -> dict[int, list[str]]:
    found = {}
    for entry in pathlib.Path("/proc").iterdir():
        if not entry.name.isdigit():
            continue
        with contextlib.suppress(OSError, IndexError):
            fields = (entry / "stat").read_text(encoding="utf-8").rsplit(")", 1)[1]
            found[int(entry.name)] = fields.split()
    return found


def quiet(*, arguments: list[str]) -> None:
    subprocess.run(arguments, capture_output=True, check=False)


def release(*, device: str) -> None:
    quiet(arguments=["blockdev", "--setrw", device])
    quiet(arguments=["losetup", "-d", device])


def remove_block_nodes() -> None:
    shutil.rmtree("/dev/block", ignore_errors=True)
    for folder, _, names in os.walk(DEVICES):
        for name in names:
            path = pathlib.Path(folder) / name
            with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                if stat.S_ISBLK(path.lstat().st_mode):
                    path.unlink()


def snapshot() -> None:
    entries = []
    with (DISK / "mmcblk0.img").open("rb") as disk:
        disk.seek(SECTOR)
        header = disk.read(SECTOR)
        entries_at, count, size = struct.unpack("<QII", header[72:88])
        disk.seek(entries_at * SECTOR)
        raw = disk.read(count * size)
    for index in range(count):
        entry = raw[index * size : (index + 1) * size]
        if entry[:16] == bytes(16):
            continue
        first, last, attributes = struct.unpack("<QQQ", entry[32:56])
        name = entry[56:128].decode("utf-16le").rstrip("\0")
        print(
            f"table {index + 1} {name} {first}-{last} type={entry[:16].hex()}"
            f" attributes={attributes:x}"
        )
        entries.append(f"guid {index + 1} {name} {entry[16:32].hex()}")
    print("\n".join(entries))
    MOUNT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    nodes = sorted(BY_NAME.iterdir()) + [
        pathlib.Path(f"/dev/block/mmcblk0{side}") for side in ("boot0", "boot1", "rpmb")
    ]
    for path in nodes:
        if not path.exists():
            print(f"part {path.name} missing")
            continue
        with path.open("rb") as file:
            file.seek(1080)
            magic = file.read(2)
        if (
            magic == b"\x53\xef"
            and not subprocess.run(
                ["mount", "-o", "ro", "-t", "ext4", str(path), str(MOUNT)],
                capture_output=True,
                check=False,
            ).returncode
        ):
            try:
                digest, lines = tree(path=MOUNT)
            finally:
                quiet(arguments=["umount", str(MOUNT)])
            print(f"part {path.name} ext4 {digest} files={len(lines)}")
            for line in lines:
                print(f"file {path.name} {line}")
        else:
            with path.open("rb") as file:
                print(f"part {path.name} raw {md5(data=file.read())}")


def table() -> list[tuple[int, str, int, int]]:
    with (DISK / "mmcblk0.img").open("rb") as disk:
        disk.seek(SECTOR)
        header = disk.read(SECTOR)
        entries_at, count, size = struct.unpack("<QII", header[72:88])
        disk.seek(entries_at * SECTOR)
        raw = disk.read(count * size)
    found = []
    for index in range(count):
        entry = raw[index * size : (index + 1) * size]
        if entry[:16] == bytes(16):
            continue
        first, last = struct.unpack("<QQ", entry[32:48])
        name = entry[56:128].decode("utf-16le").rstrip("\0")
        found.append((index + 1, name, first, last - first + 1))
    return found


def top_mount(*, target: str) -> str:
    kind = ""
    for line in (
        pathlib.Path("/proc/self/mountinfo").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    ):
        fields = line.split()
        if fields[4] == target:
            kind = fields[fields.index("-") + 1]
    return kind


def tree(*, path: pathlib.Path) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    lines = []
    for item in sorted(path.rglob("*")):
        relative = item.relative_to(path)
        if relative.parts[0] == "lost+found":
            continue
        status = item.lstat()
        if item.is_symlink():
            detail = "-> " + str(item.readlink())
        elif item.is_file():
            detail = md5(data=item.read_bytes())
        else:
            detail = "dir"
        lines.append(
            f"{relative} {status.st_mode:o} {status.st_uid}:{status.st_gid} {detail}"
        )
    return md5(data="\n".join(lines).encode()), lines


def unmount_all() -> None:
    mounted = pathlib.Path("/proc/mounts").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    targets = [
        line.split()[1]
        for line in mounted
        if line.split()[0].startswith(("/dev/block", "/dev/loop"))
        or line.split()[1] in {"/proc/partitions", SYSFS, "/sys/class/block"}
        or line.split()[1].startswith(f"{TEMPORARY}/")
    ]
    for target in reversed(targets):
        quiet(arguments=["umount", "-l", target])


def wait_gone(*, pid: int, seconds: float) -> None:
    status = pathlib.Path(f"/proc/{pid}/stat")
    deadline = time.monotonic() + seconds
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        try:
            if status.read_text(encoding="utf-8").rsplit(")", 1)[1].split()[0] == "Z":
                return
        except OSError:
            return
        time.sleep(0.05)


main()

```

`simulator/device/sim-emmc`:

```
#!/usr/bin/python3
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import struct
import sys
from typing import BinaryIO

AREAS = ("mmcblk0", "boot0", "boot1", "rpmb")
BLOCK = 512
HEADER = struct.Struct(">cBQI")


def exactly(*, length: int, stream: BinaryIO) -> bytes:
    data = b""
    while len(data) < length:
        piece = stream.read(length - len(data))
        if not piece:
            return data
        data += piece
    return data


def main() -> None:
    source, sink = sys.stdin.buffer, sys.stdout.buffer
    files = [os.open(f"/sim/disk/{area}.img", os.O_RDWR) for area in AREAS]
    while True:
        header = exactly(length=HEADER.size, stream=source)
        if len(header) < HEADER.size:
            return
        operation, area, first, count = HEADER.unpack(header)
        size = os.fstat(files[area]).st_size
        inside = (first + count) * BLOCK <= size
        if operation == b"r":
            data = b""
            if inside:
                data = os.pread(files[area], count * BLOCK, first * BLOCK)
            sink.write((b"k" if inside else b"e") + data.ljust(count * BLOCK, b"\0"))
        elif operation == b"w":
            data = exactly(length=count * BLOCK, stream=source)
            if inside:
                os.pwrite(files[area], data, first * BLOCK)
                os.fsync(files[area])
            sink.write(b"k" if inside else b"e")
        sink.flush()


main()

```

`simulator/device/sim-flavours`:

```
#!/bin/sh
set -e
busybox=$(/opt/dot/busybox --list)
toybox=$(/opt/dot/toybox)
for manifest in /sim/manifests/*; do
    flavour=/sim/flavours/${manifest##*/}
    mkdir -p "$flavour"
    : > "$flavour.missing"
    while read -r path names; do
        for name in $names; do
            case $path in
                -) provided= ;;
                */busybox) provided="$busybox busybox" ;;
                */toybox) provided="$toybox toybox" ;;
                *) provided=$name ;;
            esac
            if printf '%s\n' $provided | grep -qxF -- "$name"; then
                ln -s "$path" "$flavour/$name"
            else
                echo "$name" >> "$flavour.missing"
            fi
        done
    done < "$manifest"
done

`simulator/device/sim-pty`:

```
#!/usr/bin/python3
import contextlib
import os
import pty
import select
import signal
import sys

CHUNK = 65536
HELD = 1 << 20
POLL_MILLISECONDS = 50


def hang_up(*, master: int, pid: int) -> None:
    with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
        os.killpg(pid, signal.SIGHUP)
    os.close(master)
    os.waitpid(pid, 0)


def relay(*, master: int, pid: int) -> None:
    os.set_blocking(master, False)
    pending = b""
    poller = select.poll()
    while os.waitpid(pid, os.WNOHANG)[0] != pid:
        poller.register(0, select.POLLIN if len(pending) < HELD else 0)
        poller.register(master, select.POLLIN | (select.POLLOUT if pending else 0))
        events = dict(poller.poll(POLL_MILLISECONDS))
        if events.get(master, 0) & select.POLLIN:
            with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                os.write(1, os.read(master, CHUNK))
        if pending and events.get(master, 0) & select.POLLOUT:
            with contextlib.suppress(BlockingIOError):
                pending = pending[os.write(master, pending) :]
        if events.get(0, 0) & select.POLLIN:
            data = os.read(0, CHUNK)
            if not data:
                hang_up(master=master, pid=pid)
                return
            pending += data
        elif events.get(0, 0) & (select.POLLHUP | select.POLLERR):
            hang_up(master=master, pid=pid)
            return
    with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
        data = os.read(master, CHUNK)
        while data:
            os.write(1, data)
            data = os.read(master, CHUNK)


child, terminal = pty.fork()
if child == 0:
    os.execv("/sim/path/sh", ["sh", "-c", sys.argv[1]])  # ruff: ignore[start-process-with-no-shell]
relay(master=terminal, pid=child)
sys.exit(0)

```

`simulator/device/sim-stdin`:

```
#!/usr/bin/python3
import contextlib
import hashlib
import os
import select
import subprocess
import sys

CHUNK = 1 << 20
SIGNALLED = 128


def main() -> int:
    marker = sys.argv[1]
    process = subprocess.Popen(sys.argv[2:], stdin=subprocess.PIPE)
    sink = process.stdin.fileno()
    os.set_blocking(sink, False)
    watcher = os.pidfd_open(process.pid)
    digest = hashlib.md5(usedforsecurity=False)
    received = 0
    pending = b""
    ended = broken = False
    while True:
        poller = select.poll()
        poller.register(watcher, select.POLLIN)
        if not (ended or pending):
            poller.register(0, select.POLLIN)
        if pending:
            poller.register(sink, select.POLLOUT)
        events = dict(poller.poll())
        if events.get(watcher):
            break
        if events.get(0):
            data = os.read(0, CHUNK)
            received += len(data)
            digest.update(data)
            ended = not data
            pending = b"" if broken else data
        if pending and events.get(sink):
            try:
                pending = pending[os.write(sink, pending) :]
            except BlockingIOError:
                pass
            except BrokenPipeError:
                pending = b""
                broken = True
        if ended and not pending and not process.stdin.closed:
            with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                process.stdin.close()
    status = process.wait()
    with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
        os.write(1, f"{marker} {received} {digest.hexdigest()}\n".encode())
    return SIGNALLED - status if status < 0 else status


sys.exit(main())

```

`simulator/device/sim-sysfs`:

```
#!/usr/bin/python3
from __future__ import annotations

import errno
import re
import stat
import subprocess
import sys

import fusepy as fuse

FILE_SIZE = 4096
NUMBER = re.compile(r"0[xX][0-9a-fA-F]+|0[0-7]*|[1-9][0-9]*")
SIDES = ("boot0", "boot1")


class Sysfs(fuse.Operations):
    def __init__(self, *, sizes: dict[str, int]) -> None:
        self.files = {f"/mmcblk0{side}/size": f"{sizes[side]}\n" for side in SIDES}

    def getattr(self, path: str, *_: object) -> dict:
        if path == "/" or path in {f"/mmcblk0{side}" for side in SIDES}:
            return {"st_mode": stat.S_IFDIR | 0o755, "st_nlink": 2}
        if path.endswith("/force_ro") and path.removesuffix("/force_ro") in {
            f"/mmcblk0{side}" for side in SIDES
        }:
            return {
                "st_mode": stat.S_IFREG | 0o644,
                "st_nlink": 1,
                "st_size": FILE_SIZE,
            }
        if path not in self.files:
            raise fuse.FuseOSError(errno.ENOENT)
        return {"st_mode": stat.S_IFREG | 0o444, "st_nlink": 1, "st_size": FILE_SIZE}

    def read(self, path: str, size: int, offset: int, *_: object) -> bytes:
        if path.endswith("/force_ro"):
            node = "/dev/block" + path.removesuffix("/force_ro")
            said = subprocess.run(
                ["blockdev", "--getro", node],
                capture_output=True,
                check=True,
                text=True,
            ).stdout.strip()
            return f"{said}\n".encode()[offset : offset + size]
        return self.files[path].encode()[offset : offset + size]

    def readdir(self, path: str, *_: object) -> list[str]:  # ruff: ignore[no-self-use]
        if path == "/":
            return [".", "..", *(f"mmcblk0{side}" for side in SIDES)]
        return [".", "..", "force_ro", "size"]

    def truncate(self, path: str, length: int, fh: int | None = None) -> None:
        pass

    def write(self, path: str, data: bytes, *_: object) -> int:  # ruff: ignore[no-self-use]
        if not path.endswith("/force_ro"):
            raise fuse.FuseOSError(errno.EACCES)
        value = simple_strtoul(text=data.decode(errors="replace"))
        if value is None:
            raise fuse.FuseOSError(errno.EINVAL)
        node = "/dev/block" + path.removesuffix("/force_ro")
        subprocess.run(
            ["blockdev", "--setro" if value else "--setrw", node], check=True
        )
        return len(data)


def simple_strtoul(*, text: str) -> int | None:
    found = NUMBER.match(text)
    if found is None:
        return None
    word = found.group()
    if word[:2].lower() == "0x":
        return int(word, 16)
    if word.startswith("0"):
        return int(word, 8)
    return int(word)


fuse.FUSE(
    Sysfs(sizes=dict(zip(SIDES, map(int, sys.argv[2:4])))),
    sys.argv[1],
    allow_other=True,
    default_permissions=True,
    direct_io=True,
    foreground=True,
    nonempty=True,
)

```

`simulator/device/toolbox`:

```
#!/usr/bin/python3
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import os
import pathlib
import sys
import time

CHUNK = 4096
ROW = 16


def getevent(*, arguments: list[str]) -> int:
    del arguments
    while True:
        time.sleep(3600)


def hd(*, arguments: list[str]) -> int:  # ruff: ignore[complex-structure]
    options = {"b": -1, "c": 0, "r": 0}
    words = list(arguments)
    while words and words[0].startswith("-") and len(words[0]) > 1:
        flag = words.pop(0)
        if flag[1] not in options:
            print(f"hd: invalid option -{flag[1]}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
        value = flag[2:] or (words.pop(0) if words else "")
        options[flag[1]] = int(value, 0)
    base, count = options["b"], options["c"]
    if len(words) != 1:
        print("Usage: hd [-b base] [-c count] [-r delay] file", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    try:
        descriptor = os.open(words[0], os.O_RDONLY)
    except OSError as error:
        print(f"could not open {words[0]}, {error.strerror}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    position = 0
    if base >= 0:
        os.lseek(descriptor, base, os.SEEK_SET)
        position = base
    total = 0
    while True:
        length = CHUNK
        if count > 0 and base + count - position < length:
            length = base + count - position
        data = os.read(descriptor, length)
        if not data:
            break
        line = 0
        for index, byte in enumerate(data):
            if index % ROW == 0:
                sys.stdout.write(f"{position + index:08x}: ")
            line += byte
            total += byte
            sys.stdout.write(f"{byte:02x} ")
            if index % ROW == ROW - 1 or index == len(data) - 1:
                sys.stdout.write(f"s {line:x}\n")
                line = 0
        position += len(data)
        if position == base + count:
            break
    sys.stdout.write(f"sum {total:x}\n")
    return 0


def md5(*, arguments: list[str]) -> int:
    if not arguments:
        print("md5 file ...", file=sys.stderr)
        return 255
    status = 0
    for path in arguments:
        try:
            descriptor = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY)
        except OSError as error:
            print(f"could not open {path}, {error.strerror}", file=sys.stderr)
            status = 1
            continue
        digest = hashlib.md5(usedforsecurity=False)
        try:
            while data := os.read(descriptor, CHUNK):
                digest.update(data)
        except OSError as error:
            print(f"could not read {path}, {error.strerror}", file=sys.stderr)
            status = 1
            continue
        finally:
            os.close(descriptor)
        print(f"{digest.hexdigest()}  {path}")
    return status


def quiet(*, arguments: list[str]) -> int:
    del arguments
    return 0


COMMANDS = {
    "getevent": getevent,
    "hd": hd,
    "log": quiet,
    "md5": md5,
    "start": quiet,
    "stop": quiet,
}
sys.exit(COMMANDS[pathlib.Path(sys.argv[0]).name](arguments=sys.argv[1:]))

```

`simulator/device/toolbox-dd`:

#!/bin/sh
output=
for operand in "$@"; do
    case $operand in
        conv=*) echo "dd: conv option disabled" >&2; exit 1 ;;
        of=*) output=${operand#of=} ;;
    esac
done
if [ -b "$output" ]; then
    exec /opt/dot/toybox dd "$@" conv=notrunc
fi
exec /opt/dot/toybox dd "$@"
