`simulator/device/twrp`:

```
#!/usr/bin/python3
import pathlib
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import zipfile

BY_NAME = pathlib.Path("/dev/block/platform/mtk-msdc.0/by-name")
RUN = pathlib.Path("/sim/run")
WITH_TARGET = 3


def install(*, path: str) -> int:
    print(f"Installing zip file '{path}'")
    try:
        archive = zipfile.ZipFile(path)
    except (OSError, zipfile.BadZipFile):
        print("Invalid zip file format!")
        return 1
    names = set(archive.namelist())
    active = (RUN / "active").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    if "META-INF/com/android/metadata" in names:
        metadata = dict(
            line.partition("=")[::2]
            for line in archive
            .read("META-INF/com/android/metadata")
            .decode()
            .splitlines()
        )
        build = metadata.get("post-build", "unknown")
        slot = "b" if active == "a" else "a"
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            prop = pathlib.Path(folder) / "build.prop"
            prop.write_text(
                f"ro.build.version.name=Fire OS 6 ({build})\n"
                f"ro.build.fingerprint={build}\n",
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            subprocess.run(
                [
                    "mke2fs",
                    "-q",
                    "-F",
                    "-t",
                    "ext4",
                    "-d",
                    folder,
                    str(BY_NAME / f"system_{slot}"),
                ],
                capture_output=True,
                check=True,
            )
        (RUN / f"root-adb-{slot}").unlink(missing_ok=True)
        (RUN / "active").write_text(slot + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        print("Installing update...")
    else:
        (RUN / f"root-adb-{active}").touch()
        print("Installing boot-root...")
    print("Done processing script file")
    return 0


def main() -> int:
    if len(sys.argv) == WITH_TARGET and sys.argv[1] == "install":
        return install(path=sys.argv[2])
    if len(sys.argv) == WITH_TARGET and sys.argv[1] == "wipe":
        return wipe(part=sys.argv[2])
    print("usage: twrp install <zip> | wipe <partition>", file=sys.stderr)
    return 1


def wipe(*, part: str) -> int:
    name = {"data": "userdata"}.get(part, part)
    subprocess.run(
        ["mke2fs", "-q", "-F", "-t", "ext4", str(BY_NAME / name)],
        capture_output=True,
        check=True,
    )
    print(f"Wiping {part}... Done.")
    return 0


sys.exit(main())

```

`simulator/dot.py`:

```py
"""Simulate an Echo Dot (2nd Generation) for firebreak.

python3 simulator/dot.py create [--cache DIR] [--runner PID]
    [--start fastboot|no-light|stock-booted]
python3 simulator/dot.py plug
python3 simulator/dot.py status
python3 simulator/dot.py snapshot
python3 simulator/dot.py table FILE
python3 simulator/dot.py halt
python3 simulator/dot.py destroy
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import contextlib
import fcntl
import hashlib
import json
import os
import pathlib
import select
import shlex
import shutil
import signal
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import uuid
import zipfile
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from collections.abc import Generator
    from hashlib import _Hash as Hash
    from typing import BinaryIO

try:
    from firebreak_sim_ports import busy
except ImportError:
    busy = contextlib.nullcontext

ADB_DEVICE_MODES = frozenset({"device", "recovery", "unauthorized"})
ADB_PAIRED_FLAGS = frozenset({"-H", "-P", "-s", "-t"})
ADB_FLAGS = frozenset({"-d", "-e"}) | ADB_PAIRED_FLAGS
ADB_SHELL_MODES = frozenset({"device", "recovery"})
ADB_SHELL_OPTIONS = frozenset("ntTx")
ADB_UNAUTHORIZED_VERBS = frozenset({"exec-out", "get-state", "push", "reboot", "shell"})
BOOT0_SECTORS = 2048
BOOT_SECONDS = float(os.environ.get("FIREBREAK_SIM_BOOT_SECONDS", "3"))
BY_NAME = "/dev/block/platform/mtk-msdc.0/by-name"
DETACH_SHELL = "mkdir -p /sim/run && cat > /sim/run/loops && /sim/bin/sim-boot detach"
DISK_BYTES = 3917479936
DOWNLOAD_SUFFIXES = frozenset({".bin", ".img", ".whl", ".zip"})
FASTBOOT_FLAGS = frozenset({"-S", "-s"})
FASTBOOT_INTO = frozenset({"bootloader", "fastboot"})
FASTBOOT_REBOOTS = {
    "oem reboot-recovery": "recovery",
    "reboot": "system",
    "reboot bootloader": "bootloader",
    "reboot recovery": "recovery",
    "reboot-bootloader": "bootloader",
}
FASTBOOT_SPARSE_UNITS = {"G": 1 << 30, "K": 1 << 10, "M": 1 << 20}
HALT_SECONDS = 10
HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
IMAGE = "firebreak-sim"
INSIDE_PATH = "/sim/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin"
LEGACY_SERVICE_BYTES = 4096
LK_FIREOS5 = "f379dba-20170906_000423"
LK_FIREOS6 = "63cb91b-20221007_072309"
LOCKED = "the command you input is restricted on locked hw"
MTP_PROPS = ("ro.twrp.version", "sys.usb.config")
PUSH_SHELL = 'cat > "$1" && { [ "$2" = 0 ] || chmod 666 "$1"; }'
SECTOR = 512
SERIAL = "G090SIM000000001"
SHELL_UID = 2000
SIDE_PARTITIONS = frozenset({"boot0", "boot1"})
SIM_CACHE = pathlib.Path(
    os.environ.get("FIREBREAK_SIM_CACHE")
    or pathlib.Path.home() / ".cache" / "firebreak-sim-cache"
)
STDIN_CHUNK = 1 << 20
STDIN_POLL_SECONDS = 0.1
STOCK_LK_SIZE = 241664
STOCK_TABLE = (
    ("kb", 2048, 2048),
    ("dkb", 4096, 2048),
    ("lk_a", 32768, 2048),
    ("tee1", 49152, 10240),
    ("lk_b", 65536, 2048),
    ("tee2", 81920, 10240),
    ("expdb", 98304, 20480),
    ("misc", 118784, 1025),
    ("persist", 131072, 32768),
    ("boot_a", 163840, 32768),
    ("boot_b", 196608, 32768),
    ("recovery", 229376, 32768),
    ("system_a", 294912, 1572864),
    ("system_b", 1867776, 1572864),
    ("cache", 3440640, 1605632),
    ("userdata", 5046272, 2605023),
)
TABLE_BACKUP_SECTORS = 33
TABLE_PRIMARY_SECTORS = 34
TOOL_CACHE = "firebreak"
TWRPS = {"bboe": "3.7.0_9-bboe2", "v1": "3.2.3-0", "v2": "3.7.0_9-0"}
USB_CUT_STATUS = 255
USB_DROP_SECONDS = 1.0
USB_SWITCH_SECONDS = 5.0
WAIT_FOR_ANY = frozenset({"any", "usb-device"})
WORK = pathlib.Path(
    os.environ.get("FIREBREAK_SIM") or pathlib.Path.home() / ".cache" / "firebreak-sim"
)
CLOCK = WORK / "clock.json"
STATE = WORK / "state.json"
TRACE = WORK / "trace.jsonl"


def adb(*, arguments: list[str]) -> int:  # ruff: ignore[complex-structure, too-many-branches, too-many-return-statements]
    entry = {"argv": arguments, "tool": "adb"}
    while arguments and arguments[0] in ADB_FLAGS:
        arguments = arguments[2 if arguments[0] in ADB_PAIRED_FLAGS else 1 :]
    verb = arguments[0] if arguments else ""
    rest = arguments[1:]
    state = current()
    entry["mode"] = state["mode"]
    if verb == "push" and rest and pathlib.Path(rest[0]).is_file():
        entry["md5"] = md5(data=pathlib.Path(rest[0]).read_bytes())
    record(entry=entry)
    if verb == "version":
        print("Android Debug Bridge version 1.0.41\nVersion 37.0.0-sim")
        return 0
    if verb == "devices":
        print("List of devices attached")
        if seen(state=state) in ADB_DEVICE_MODES:
            extra = " usb:1-1 product:biscuit model:AEOBC device:biscuit transport_id:1"
            print(f"{state['serial']}\t{state['mode']}{extra if '-l' in rest else ''}")
        print()
        return 0
    if verb.startswith("wait-for-"):
        wanted = verb[len("wait-for-") :]
        while seen(state=state) != wanted and not (
            wanted in WAIT_FOR_ANY and seen(state=state) in ADB_SHELL_MODES
        ):
            time.sleep(0.5)
            state = current()
        return 0
    if state["mode"] == "unauthorized" and verb in ADB_UNAUTHORIZED_VERBS:
        print("error: device unauthorized.", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    if seen(state=state) not in ADB_SHELL_MODES:
        print("error: no devices/emulators found", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    if verb == "get-state":
        print(state["mode"])
        return 0
    if verb == "reboot":
        request_reboot(into=rest[0] if rest else "system")
        return 0
    if verb == "push":
        return push(local=pathlib.Path(rest[0]), remote=rest[1], state=state)
    if verb in {"exec-out", "shell"}:
        return shell(entry=entry, rest=rest, state=state, verb=verb)
    print(f"adb: unknown command {verb}", file=sys.stderr)
    return 1


def boot(*, into: str, state: dict) -> None:
    sim_boot(action="boot", state=state)
    spec = {
        "prefixes": {
            "lk_a": sorted({size for size, _ in state["known"]["lk"].values()}),
            "recovery": sorted({
                size for size, _ in state["known"]["recovery"].values()
            }),
        }
    }
    facts = json.loads(sim_boot(action="facts", spec=spec, state=state))
    outcome = decide(facts=facts, into=into, state=state)
    if outcome.get("consumed"):
        inside(
            command=f"dd if=/dev/zero of={BY_NAME}/{outcome['consumed']} bs=16"
            " count=1 conv=notrunc 2>/dev/null",
            state=state,
        )
    props = dict(outcome.get("props", {}))
    later = {}
    if outcome["mode"] == "recovery":
        later = {key: props.pop(key) for key in MTP_PROPS}
        props["sys.usb.config"] = "adb"
    if "flavour" in outcome:
        sim_boot(
            action="enter",
            spec={
                "flavour": outcome["flavour"],
                "props": props,
                "twrp": outcome.get("twrp"),
                "uid": outcome["uid"],
            },
            state=state,
        )
    switch = time.time() + USB_SWITCH_SECONDS
    state.update(
        booting=None,
        mode=outcome["mode"],
        outcome=outcome,
        shell_protocol=outcome.get("shell_protocol", True),
        usb={"at": switch, "props": later, "until": switch + USB_DROP_SECONDS}
        if later
        else None,
        vars=outcome.get("vars", {}),
    )
    record(entry={"event": "boot", "into": into, "outcome": outcome})
    if outcome["mode"] == "bootrom":
        start_bootrom()


def build_prop(*, key: str, text: str | None) -> str:
    for line in (text or "").splitlines():
        if line.startswith(key + "="):
            return line.split("=", 1)[1]
    return ""


def clone(*, source: pathlib.Path, target: pathlib.Path) -> None:
    if sys.platform != "darwin":
        subprocess.run(["cp", "--reflink=auto", str(source), str(target)], check=True)
        return
    if subprocess.run(
        ["cp", "-c", str(source), str(target)], capture_output=True, check=False
    ).returncode:
        target.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        shutil.copyfile(source, target)


def create(*, cache: pathlib.Path, runner: int, start: str) -> None:
    refuse_real_caches()
    previous = load().get("runner") if STATE.exists() else None
    if previous and previous != runner and is_runner(pid=previous):
        message = f"run.sh (pid {previous}) still runs this Dot in {WORK}"
        raise SystemExit(message)
    destroy()
    WORK.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
    CLOCK.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    TRACE.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    docker(arguments=["build", "-q", "-t", IMAGE, str(HERE)], capture_output=True)
    name = f"firebreak-sim-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
    state = {
        "cache": str(cache),
        "container": name,
        "known": known_images(cache=cache),
        "mode": "none",
        "outcome": {},
        "runner": runner,
        "serial": SERIAL,
        "vars": {},
    }
    save(state=state)
    docker(
        arguments=[
            "run",
            "-d",
            "--init",
            "--network",
            "none",
            "--privileged",
            "--name",
            name,
            IMAGE,
        ],
        capture_output=True,
    )
    disk_guid = uuid.uuid5(uuid.NAMESPACE_DNS, "firebreak-sim.disk")
    table = " ".join(
        f"-n {number}:{first}:{first + sectors - 1} -c {number}:{part}"
        f" -t {number}:8300"
        f" -u {number}:{uuid.uuid5(uuid.NAMESPACE_DNS, 'firebreak-sim.' + part)}"
        for number, (part, first, sectors) in enumerate(STOCK_TABLE, start=1)
    )
    inside(
        command="mkdir -p /sim/disk /sim/run"
        f" && truncate -s {DISK_BYTES} /sim/disk/mmcblk0.img"
        " && for s in boot0 boot1 rpmb; do truncate -s 1048576 /sim/disk/$s.img;"
        " done"
        f" && sgdisk -o -U {disk_guid} {table} /sim/disk/mmcblk0.img >/dev/null"
        " && echo a > /sim/run/active",
        state=state,
    )
    sim_boot(action="boot", state=state)
    stock = {
        "/dev/block/mmcblk0boot0": (
            b"EMMC_BOOT".ljust(SECTOR, b"\0") + b"BRLYT\0SIM STOCK PRELOADER"
        ).ljust(262144, b"\x5a"),
        "/dev/block/mmcblk0rpmb": stock_rpmb(),
        f"{BY_NAME}/lk_a": stock_lk(),
        f"{BY_NAME}/lk_b": stock_lk(),
        f"{BY_NAME}/recovery": b"ANDROID!SIM STOCK RECOVERY".ljust(1 << 20, b"\0"),
    }
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
        local = pathlib.Path(folder) / "image"
        for node, data in stock.items():
            local.write_bytes(data)
            write_image(local=local, node=node, state=state)
    inside(
        command="mkdir -p /sim/stock"
        " && printf 'ro.build.version.name=Fire OS 6.5.7.4 (stock 8146)\\n'"
        " > /sim/stock/build.prop"
        " && i=0 && for p in system_a userdata cache; do i=$((i + 1));"
        " E2FSPROGS_FAKE_TIME=1700000000 mke2fs -q -F -t ext4"
        " -U 5eed0000-0000-4000-8000-$(printf %012x $i)"
        " -E hash_seed=5eed0000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000"
        f" $( [ $p = system_a ] && echo -d /sim/stock ) {BY_NAME}/$p; done",
        state=state,
    )
    make_venv()
    link_downloads(cache=cache)
    if start == "no-light":
        inside(
            command="blockdev --setrw /dev/block/mmcblk0boot0 && dd if=/dev/zero"
            f" of=/dev/block/mmcblk0boot0 bs=512 count={BOOT0_SECTORS} 2>/dev/null"
            + protect(node="/dev/block/mmcblk0boot0"),
            state=state,
        )
        record(entry={"event": "unplugged"})
    else:
        boot(into="bootloader" if start == "fastboot" else "system", state=state)
    save(state=state)
    print(f"{name}: {state['mode']}")


def current() -> dict:
    with locked():
        state = load()
        settle(state=state)
        return state


def decide(*, facts: dict, into: str, state: dict) -> dict:  # ruff: ignore[complex-structure, too-many-return-statements, too-many-branches]
    known = state["known"]
    lk = identify(prefixes=facts["prefixes"].get("lk_a", {}), table=known["lk"])
    none = {"mode": "none", "why": ""}
    if not facts["preloader"]:
        return {"mode": "bootrom", "why": "boot0 holds no preloader"}
    if lk is None:
        return {**none, "why": "lk_a holds no LK this simulator knows"}
    description = LK_FIREOS5 if lk == "v1" else LK_FIREOS6
    marker = (lk == "v1" and facts.get("expdb_fastboot")) or (
        lk == "v2" and facts.get("misc_fastboot")
    )
    if into in FASTBOOT_INTO or marker:
        return {
            "consumed": "expdb" if lk == "v1" and facts.get("expdb_fastboot") else None,
            "lk": lk,
            "mode": "fastboot",
            "vars": {
                "lk_build_desc": description,
                "product": "BISCUIT",
                "unlock_status": "false" if lk == "stock" else "true",
            },
        }
    slot = facts["active"] or "a"
    if into == "recovery":
        if lk == "stock":
            return {**none, "why": "a locked LK refuses an unsigned recovery"}
        twrp = identify(
            prefixes=facts["prefixes"].get("recovery", {}), table=known["recovery"]
        )
        if twrp is None:
            return {**none, "why": "recovery holds no TWRP this simulator knows"}
        return {
            "flavour": "twrp-3.2.3" if twrp == "v1" else "twrp-3.7.0",
            "lk": lk,
            "mode": "recovery",
            "props": {
                "ro.boot.lk_build_desc": description,
                "ro.boot.slot_suffix": f"_{slot}",
                "ro.twrp.version": TWRPS[twrp],
                "sys.usb.config": "mtp,adb",
            },
            "shell_protocol": twrp != "v1",
            "twrp": twrp,
            "uid": 0,
        }
    if lk == "v1":
        slot = "a"
    system = facts.get(f"system_{slot}") or {}
    version = build_prop(key="ro.build.version.name", text=system.get("build"))
    if version.startswith("Fire OS 5"):
        if lk != "v1":
            return {**none, "why": f"{lk}'s LK hangs booting a Fire OS 5 kernel"}
        if "if true; then" not in system.get("flags", ""):
            return {**none, "why": "Fire OS 5 booted with adb off"}
        return {
            "flavour": "fire-os-5",
            "lk": lk,
            "mode": "device",
            "props": {
                "ro.boot.lk_build_desc": description,
                "ro.boot.slot_suffix": f"_{slot}",
                "ro.build.version.name": version,
                "sim.su": "1" if facts["magisk"] else "0",
                "sys.boot_completed": "1",
                "sys.usb.config": "mtp,adb",
            },
            "shell_protocol": False,
            "uid": SHELL_UID,
        }
    if version.startswith("Fire OS 6"):
        if lk == "v1":
            return {**none, "why": "amonet v1.1.0's LK does not boot Fire OS 6"}
        if lk == "v2" and facts.get(f"root_adb_{slot}"):
            return {
                "flavour": "fire-os-6",
                "lk": lk,
                "mode": "device",
                "props": {
                    "ro.boot.lk_build_desc": description,
                    "ro.boot.slot_suffix": f"_{slot}",
                    "ro.build.version.name": version,
                    "sim.su": "0",
                    "sys.boot_completed": "1",
                },
                "uid": 0,
            }
        return {"lk": lk, "mode": "unauthorized"}
    return {**none, "why": f"system_{slot} holds no OS this simulator knows"}


def destroy() -> None:
    halt()
    with locked():
        if not STATE.exists():
            return
        state = load()
        pid = state.get("bootrom_pid")
        if pid and is_bootrom(pid=pid):
            with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                os.kill(pid, signal.SIGTERM)
        detach_loops(container=state["container"])
        docker(
            arguments=["rm", "-f", state["container"]], capture_output=True, check=False
        )
        STATE.unlink()


def detach_loops(*, container: str) -> None:
    running = docker(
        arguments=["inspect", "-f", "{{.State.Running}}", container],
        capture_output=True,
        check=False,
        text=True,
    )
    if running.returncode:
        warn(
            text=f"container {container} is gone, so its loop devices could not be"
            " detached. losetup -a in a privileged container lists them."
        )
        return
    if running.stdout.strip() != "true":
        docker(arguments=["start", container], capture_output=True, check=False)
    detached = docker(
        arguments=["exec", container, "/sim/bin/sim-boot", "detach"],
        capture_output=True,
        check=False,
        text=True,
    )
    if not detached.returncode:
        return
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
        records = pathlib.Path(folder) / "loops"
        copied = docker(
            arguments=["cp", f"{container}:/sim/run/loops", str(records)],
            capture_output=True,
            check=False,
        )
        if copied.returncode:
            warn(
                text=f"the loop records of container {container} could not be read"
                f" ({detached.stderr.strip()}). losetup -a in a privileged container"
                " lists the loops."
            )
            return
        with records.open("rb") as source:
            helped = docker(
                arguments=[
                    "run",
                    "--rm",
                    "-i",
                    "--network",
                    "none",
                    "--privileged",
                    IMAGE,
                    "/bin/sh",
                    "-c",
                    DETACH_SHELL,
                ],
                capture_output=True,
                check=False,
                stdin=source,
                text=True,
            )
        if helped.returncode:
            warn(
                text=f"the loop devices of container {container} are still attached"
                f" ({helped.stderr.strip() or detached.stderr.strip()}). Records:"
                f" {'; '.join(records.read_text().splitlines())}"
            )


def docker(
    *, arguments: list[str], check: bool = True, **options: object
) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    return subprocess.run(["docker", *arguments], check=check, **options)


def dump_table(*, path: pathlib.Path) -> None:
    state = current()
    skip = DISK_BYTES // SECTOR - TABLE_BACKUP_SECTORS
    inside(
        command="dd if=/sim/disk/mmcblk0.img bs=512"
        f" count={TABLE_PRIMARY_SECTORS} 2>/dev/null > /sim/run/table.bin"
        f" && dd if=/sim/disk/mmcblk0.img bs=512 skip={skip} 2>/dev/null"
        " >> /sim/run/table.bin",
        state=state,
    )
    docker(
        arguments=["cp", f"{state['container']}:/sim/run/table.bin", str(path)],
        capture_output=True,
    )


def erase(*, part: str, state: dict) -> int:
    if state["vars"]["unlock_status"] != "true":
        print(f"Erasing '{part}' FAILED (remote: '{LOCKED}')")
        return 1
    node = partition_node(part=part)
    size = partition_bytes(node=node, state=state)
    if size is None:
        print(f"Erasing '{part}' FAILED (remote: 'unknown partition')")
        return 1
    inside(
        command=f"blockdev --setrw {node} && dd if=/dev/zero of={node} bs=512"
        f" count={size // SECTOR} 2>/dev/null{protect(node=node)}",
        state=state,
    )
    print(f"Erasing '{part}' OKAY\nFinished. Total time: 0.050s")
    return 0


def fastboot(*, arguments: list[str]) -> int:  # ruff: ignore[complex-structure, too-many-return-statements]
    entry = {"argv": arguments, "tool": "fastboot"}
    sparse = None
    while arguments and arguments[0] in FASTBOOT_FLAGS:
        if arguments[0] == "-S" and len(arguments) > 1:
            sparse = sparse_bytes(text=arguments[1])
        arguments = arguments[2:]
    verb = arguments[0] if arguments else ""
    rest = arguments[1:]
    if verb == "--help":
        print("usage: fastboot [OPTION...] COMMAND...")
        print(
            " -S SIZE[K|M|G]             Break into sparse files no larger than SIZE."
        )
        return 0
    state = current()
    entry["mode"] = state["mode"]
    if verb == "flash" and len(rest) > 1 and pathlib.Path(rest[1]).is_file():
        entry["md5"] = md5(data=pathlib.Path(rest[1]).read_bytes())
    record(entry=entry)
    if verb == "devices":
        if state["mode"] == "fastboot":
            print(f"{state['serial']}\tfastboot")
        return 0
    while state["mode"] != "fastboot":
        time.sleep(0.5)
        state = current()
    if verb == "getvar":
        name = rest[0]
        if name in state["vars"]:
            print(f"{name}: {state['vars'][name]}\nFinished. Total time: 0.001s")
            return 0
        print(f"getvar:{name} FAILED (remote: 'GetVar Variable Not found')")
        return 1
    if verb == "flash":
        return flash(image=rest[1], part=rest[0], sparse=sparse, state=state)
    if verb == "erase":
        return erase(part=rest[0], state=state)
    into = FASTBOOT_REBOOTS.get(" ".join(arguments))
    if into:
        request_reboot(into=into)
        print("Rebooting OKAY\nFinished. Total time: 0.001s")
        return 0
    print(f"fastboot: unknown command {verb}", file=sys.stderr)
    return 1


def fastbrick(*, image: str, sparse: int | None, state: dict) -> int:
    description = state["vars"].get("lk_build_desc", "")
    wanted = "fastbrick-20221007.img" if description == LK_FIREOS6 else "fastbrick.img"
    if pathlib.Path(image).name != wanted:
        print(
            "Sending 'brick' OKAY\nWriting 'brick' FAILED (remote: 'unknown partition')"
        )
        return 1
    cache = pathlib.Path(state["cache"])
    writes = []
    with (
        zipfile.ZipFile(cache / "amonet-biscuit-v2.0.0.zip") as v2,
        tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder,
    ):
        upload = pathlib.Path(folder) / "brick"
        upload.mkdir()
        for target, suffix in (
            ("lk_a", "bin/lk.bin"),
            ("lk_b", "bin/lk.bin"),
            ("tee2", "bin/tz.img"),
            ("tee1", "bin/tee-payload.bin"),
            ("expdb", "bin/biscuit-kaeru.bin"),
            ("recovery", "bin/twrp.img"),
        ):
            (upload / target).write_bytes(member(archive=v2, suffix=suffix))
            node = f"{BY_NAME}/{target}"
            writes.append(
                f"blockdev --setrw {node} 2>/dev/null; dd if=/sim/brick/{target}"
                f" of={node} bs=512 conv=notrunc,fsync 2>/dev/null{protect(node=node)}"
            )
        docker(
            arguments=["cp", "-L", str(upload), f"{state['container']}:/sim/"],
            capture_output=True,
        )
    inside(command="; ".join([*writes, "rm -rf /sim/brick"]), state=state)
    request_reboot(delay=BOOT_SECONDS + 2, into="recovery")
    print(sending(image=pathlib.Path(image), part="brick", sparse=sparse), flush=True)
    time.sleep(3600)
    return 0


def flash(*, image: str, part: str, sparse: int | None, state: dict) -> int:
    if part == "brick":
        return fastbrick(image=image, sparse=sparse, state=state)
    node = partition_node(part=part)
    size = partition_bytes(node=node, state=state)
    refusal = (
        LOCKED
        if state["vars"]["unlock_status"] != "true"
        else "unknown partition"
        if size is None
        else "size too large"
        if pathlib.Path(image).stat().st_size > size
        else None
    )
    said = sending(image=pathlib.Path(image), part=part, sparse=sparse)
    if refusal:
        print(f"{said} OKAY\nWriting '{part}' FAILED (remote: '{refusal}')")
        return 1
    write_image(local=pathlib.Path(image), node=node, state=state)
    print(f"{said} OKAY\nWriting '{part}' OKAY\nFinished. Total time: 0.100s")
    return 0


def halt() -> None:
    if not STATE.exists():
        return
    pid = load().get("bootrom_pid")
    if not pid:
        return
    if not is_bootrom(pid=pid):
        with locked():
            state = load()
            if state.get("bootrom_pid") == pid:
                state.pop("bootrom_pid", None)
                state.pop("port", None)
                save(state=state)
        return
    with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
        os.kill(pid, signal.SIGTERM)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + HALT_SECONDS
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        try:
            os.kill(pid, 0)
        except OSError:
            return
        time.sleep(0.1)


def identify(*, prefixes: dict, table: dict) -> str | None:
    for name, (size, digest) in table.items():
        if prefixes.get(str(size)) == digest:
            return name
    return None


def identity(*, path: pathlib.Path) -> tuple[int, int] | None:
    try:
        status = path.stat()
    except OSError:
        return None
    return status.st_dev, status.st_ino


def inside(*, check: bool = True, command: str, state: dict) -> str:
    return docker(
        arguments=[
            "exec",
            "-e",
            f"PATH={INSIDE_PATH}",
            state["container"],
            "/bin/sh",
            "-c",
            command,
        ],
        capture_output=True,
        check=check,
        text=True,
    ).stdout


def is_bootrom(*, pid: int) -> bool:
    said = subprocess.run(
        ["ps", "-ww", "-o", "args=", "-p", str(pid)],
        capture_output=True,
        check=False,
        text=True,
    ).stdout.strip()
    return said.endswith(f"{HERE / 'bootrom.py'} {WORK}")


def is_runner(*, pid: int) -> bool:
    said = subprocess.run(
        ["ps", "-ww", "-o", "args=", "-p", str(pid)],
        capture_output=True,
        check=False,
        text=True,
    ).stdout.split()
    return any(pathlib.Path(word).name == "run.sh" for word in said)


def known_images(*, cache: pathlib.Path) -> dict:
    with (
        zipfile.ZipFile(cache / "amonet-biscuit-v1.1.0.zip") as v1,
        zipfile.ZipFile(cache / "amonet-biscuit-v2.0.0.zip") as v2,
    ):
        lks = {
            "v1": member(archive=v1, suffix="bin/lk.bin"),
            "v2": member(archive=v2, suffix="bin/lk.bin"),
        }
        recoveries = {
            "bboe": (cache / "twrp-3.7.0_9-bboe2-biscuit.img").read_bytes(),
            "v1": member(archive=v1, suffix="bin/twrp.img"),
            "v2": member(archive=v2, suffix="bin/twrp.img"),
        }
    lks["stock"] = stock_lk()
    return {
        "lk": {name: [len(data), md5(data=data)] for name, data in lks.items()},
        "recovery": {
            name: [len(data), md5(data=data)] for name, data in recoveries.items()
        },
    }


def link_downloads(*, cache: pathlib.Path) -> None:
    for download in sorted(cache.iterdir()):
        if not (download.is_file() and download.suffix in DOWNLOAD_SUFFIXES):
            continue
        copy = SIM_CACHE / TOOL_CACHE / download.name
        if copy.is_symlink():
            copy.unlink()
        source = download.stat()
        if copy.exists() and (copy.stat().st_size, copy.stat().st_mtime_ns) == (
            source.st_size,
            source.st_mtime_ns,
        ):
            continue
        copy.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        clone(source=download, target=copy)
        os.utime(copy, ns=(source.st_atime_ns, source.st_mtime_ns))


def load() -> dict:
    return json.loads(STATE.read_text())


@contextlib.contextmanager
def locked() -> Generator[None, None, None]:
    WORK.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
    with (WORK / "lock").open("w") as handle:
        fcntl.flock(handle, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        yield


def main() -> int:
    if sys.argv[1:2] == ["adb"]:
        return adb(arguments=sys.argv[2:])
    if sys.argv[1:2] == ["fastboot"]:
        return fastboot(arguments=sys.argv[2:])
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter
    )
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="action", required=True)
    made = commands.add_parser("create")
    made.add_argument(
        "--cache", default=str(pathlib.Path.home() / ".cache" / "firebreak")
    )
    made.add_argument("--runner", default=0, type=int)
    made.add_argument(
        "--start", choices=("fastboot", "no-light", "stock-booted"), default="fastboot"
    )
    commands.add_parser("destroy")
    commands.add_parser("halt")
    commands.add_parser("plug")
    commands.add_parser("snapshot")
    commands.add_parser("status")
    table = commands.add_parser("table")
    table.add_argument("path", type=pathlib.Path)
    options = parser.parse_args()
    if options.action == "create":
        create(
            cache=pathlib.Path(options.cache),
            runner=options.runner,
            start=options.start,
        )
    elif options.action == "destroy":
        destroy()
    elif options.action == "halt":
        halt()
    elif options.action == "plug":
        plug()
    elif options.action == "snapshot":
        print(sim_boot(action="snapshot", state=current()), end="")
    elif options.action == "status":
        state = current()
        shown = {key: state[key] for key in ("container", "mode", "outcome")}
        print(json.dumps(shown, indent=1))
    elif options.action == "table":
        dump_table(path=options.path)
    return 0


def make_venv() -> None:
    venv = WORK / "venv"
    python = venv / "bin" / "python"
    if not python.exists():
        subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, "-m", "venv", "--without-pip", str(venv)], check=True
        )
    packages = pathlib.Path(
        subprocess.run(
            [
                str(python),
                "-c",
                "import sysconfig; print(sysconfig.get_paths()['purelib'])",
            ],
            capture_output=True,
            check=True,
            text=True,
        ).stdout.strip()
    )
    (packages / "firebreak_sim_ports.py").write_text((HERE / "ports.py").read_text())
    (packages / "firebreak_sim_ports.pth").write_text("import firebreak_sim_ports\n")


def md5(*, data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.md5(data, usedforsecurity=False).hexdigest()


def member(*, archive: zipfile.ZipFile, suffix: str) -> bytes:
    for name in archive.namelist():
        if name.endswith(suffix):
            return archive.read(name)
    raise KeyError(suffix)


def partition_bytes(*, node: str, state: dict) -> int | None:
    said = inside(
        check=False,
        command=f"[ -b {node} ] && blockdev --getsize64 {node}",
        state=state,
    ).strip()
    return int(said) if said.isdigit() else None


def partition_node(*, part: str) -> str:
    if part in SIDE_PARTITIONS:
        return f"/dev/block/mmcblk0{part}"
    return f"{BY_NAME}/{part}"


def plug() -> None:
    with locked():
        state = load()
        if state["mode"] == "none" and not state.get("booting"):
            boot(into="system", state=state)
            save(state=state)
        print(state["mode"])


def protect(*, node: str) -> str:
    if node.removeprefix("/dev/block/mmcblk0") in SIDE_PARTITIONS:
        return f"; blockdev --setro {node}"
    return ""


def push(*, local: pathlib.Path, remote: str, state: dict) -> int:
    if remote.endswith("/"):
        remote += local.name
    started = time.time()
    with local.open("rb") as source:
        copied = docker(
            arguments=[
                "exec",
                "-i",
                "-u",
                user(state=state),
                state["container"],
                "/bin/sh",
                "-c",
                PUSH_SHELL,
                "push",
                remote,
                str(state["outcome"]["uid"]),
            ],
            capture_output=True,
            check=False,
            stdin=source,
        )
    if copied.returncode:
        print(
            f"adb: error: failed to copy '{local}' to '{remote}': remote couldn't"
            " create file: Permission denied"
        )
        return 1
    if switched_during(started=started, state=state):
        inside(command=f"rm -f {shlex.quote(remote)}", state=state)
        record(
            entry={
                "call": {"argv": ["push", str(local), remote], "tool": "adb"},
                "event": "cut",
            }
        )
        print(f"adb: error: failed to read copy response: EOF\n{local}: 0 files pushed")
        return 1
    size = local.stat().st_size
    print(f"{local}: 1 file pushed, 0 skipped. 40.0 MB/s ({size} bytes in 0.010s)")
    return 0


def reboot(*, delay: float = BOOT_SECONDS, into: str, state: dict) -> None:
    state.update(booting={"at": time.time() + delay, "into": into}, mode="none")
    record(entry={"event": "reboot", "into": into})


def reboot_requested(*, state: dict) -> str | None:
    said = inside(
        check=False,
        command="[ -f /sim/run/request ] && { cat /sim/run/request;"
        " : > /sim/run/request; }",
        state=state,
    ).strip()
    return said or None


def received_stdin(*, marker: str, output: bytes) -> tuple[bytes, dict | None]:
    start = output.rfind(marker.encode())
    if start < 0:
        return output, None
    end = output.find(b"\n", start) + 1 or len(output)
    count, digest = output[start:end].split()[1:3]
    return output[:start] + output[end:], {
        "bytes": int(count),
        "md5": digest.decode(),
    }


def record(*, entry: dict) -> None:
    line = json.dumps({"time": round(time.time(), 3), **entry}) + "\n"
    WORK.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
    descriptor = os.open(TRACE, os.O_APPEND | os.O_CREAT | os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)
    try:
        fcntl.flock(descriptor, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        view = memoryview(line.encode())
        while view:
            view = view[os.write(descriptor, view) :]
    finally:
        os.close(descriptor)


def refuse_real_caches() -> None:
    home = os.environ.get("HOME")
    if not home:
        message = "HOME is not set, so the real caches cannot be found"
        raise SystemExit(message)
    roots = {pathlib.Path(home) / ".cache"}
    if os.environ.get("XDG_CACHE_HOME"):
        roots.add(pathlib.Path(os.environ["XDG_CACHE_HOME"]))
    real = {
        identity(path=root / name) for root in roots for name in ("", TOOL_CACHE)
    } - {None}
    SIM_CACHE.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
    if identity(path=SIM_CACHE) in real:
        message = (
            f"FIREBREAK_SIM_CACHE must not be {SIM_CACHE}: a run would write there"
        )
        raise SystemExit(message)
    folder = SIM_CACHE / TOOL_CACHE
    if folder.is_symlink():
        message = f"{folder} is a link: a run would write where it points"
        raise SystemExit(message)
    folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    if identity(path=folder) in real:
        message = f"{folder} is a real cache: a run would write there"
        raise SystemExit(message)


def request_reboot(*, delay: float = BOOT_SECONDS, into: str) -> None:
    with locked():
        state = load()
        reboot(delay=delay, into=into, state=state)
        save(state=state)


def save(*, state: dict) -> None:
    temporary = STATE.with_suffix(".tmp")
    temporary.write_text(json.dumps(state, indent=1, sort_keys=True))
    temporary.replace(STATE)


def seen(*, state: dict) -> str:
    usb = state.get("usb")
    if usb and usb["at"] <= time.time() < usb["until"]:
        return "none"
    return state["mode"]


def sending(*, image: pathlib.Path, part: str, sparse: int | None) -> str:
    size = image.stat().st_size
    if sparse and size > sparse:
        return f"Sending sparse '{part}' 1/{-(-size // sparse)} ({sparse // 1024} KB)"
    return f"Sending '{part}' ({size // 1024} KB)"


def settle(*, state: dict) -> dict:
    booting = state.get("booting")
    if booting and time.time() >= booting["at"]:
        boot(into=booting["into"], state=state)
        save(state=state)
    usb = state.get("usb")
    if usb and usb["props"] and time.time() >= usb["at"]:
        inside(
            command=" && ".join(
                f"setprop {key} {shlex.quote(value)}"
                for key, value in usb["props"].items()
            ),
            state=state,
        )
        usb["props"] = {}
        save(state=state)
    return state


def shell(*, entry: dict, rest: list[str], state: dict, verb: str) -> int:  # ruff: ignore[complex-structure, too-many-locals]
    device_protocol = state.get("shell_protocol", True)
    if verb == "exec-out":
        command = " ".join([*rest[:1], *(shell_quote(text=word) for word in rest[1:])])
        flags: set[str] = {"n"}
        protocol = False
    else:
        parsed = shell_options(words=rest)
        if isinstance(parsed, str):
            print(f"adb shell: invalid option -- '{parsed}'", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
        flags, command = parsed
        protocol = device_protocol and "x" not in flags
        if not protocol and "T" in flags:
            print("error: device only supports allocating a pty", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
        if not protocol and len("shell:" + command) > LEGACY_SERVICE_BYTES:
            print("error: shell command too long", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
    program = (
        ["/sim/bin/sim-pty", command]
        if verb == "shell" and not device_protocol
        else ["/sim/path/sh", "-c", command]
    )
    forwarded = "n" not in flags
    marker = uuid.uuid4().hex
    if forwarded:
        program = ["/sim/bin/sim-stdin", marker, *program]
    started = time.time()
    process = subprocess.Popen(
        [
            "docker",
            "exec",
            "-i",
            "-e",
            "PATH=/sim/path",
            "-u",
            user(state=state),
            state["container"],
            *program,
        ],
        stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
    )
    if protocol and not forwarded:
        process.stdin.close()
    digest = hashlib.md5(usedforsecurity=False)
    counted = [0]
    done = threading.Event()
    feeder = threading.Thread(
        kwargs={
            "close": protocol,
            "counted": counted,
            "digest": digest,
            "done": done,
            "sink": process.stdin,
        },
        target=stream,
    )
    if forwarded:
        feeder.start()
    output = process.stdout.read()
    status = process.wait()
    done.set()
    if forwarded:
        feeder.join()
        output, received = received_stdin(marker=marker, output=output)
        record(
            entry={
                "bytes": counted[0],
                "call": {"argv": entry["argv"], "tool": "adb"},
                "event": "stdin",
                "md5": digest.hexdigest(),
                "received": received,
            }
        )
    with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
        process.stdin.close()
    requested = reboot_requested(state=state)
    if requested:
        request_reboot(into=requested)
    if switched_during(started=started, state=state):
        record(entry={"call": {"argv": [verb, *rest], "tool": "adb"}, "event": "cut"})
        sys.stdout.buffer.write(output[: len(output) // 2])
        return USB_CUT_STATUS if protocol else 0
    sys.stdout.buffer.write(output)
    return status if protocol else 0


def shell_options(*, words: list[str]) -> tuple[set[str], str] | str:
    flags = set()
    index = 0
    while index < len(words):
        word = words[index]
        if word == "--":
            index += 1
            break
        if not word.startswith("-") or word == "-":
            break
        index += 1
        letters = word[1:]
        for position, letter in enumerate(letters):
            if letter == "e":
                if position + 1 == len(letters):
                    index += 1
                break
            if letter not in ADB_SHELL_OPTIONS:
                return letter
            flags.add(letter)
    return flags, " ".join(words[index:])


def shell_quote(*, text: str) -> str:
    return "'" + text.replace("'", "'\\''") + "'"


def sim_boot(*, action: str, spec: dict | None = None, state: dict) -> str:
    arguments = ["exec", state["container"], "/sim/bin/sim-boot", action]
    if spec is not None:
        arguments.append(json.dumps(spec))
    return docker(arguments=arguments, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout


def sparse_bytes(*, text: str) -> int:
    unit = FASTBOOT_SPARSE_UNITS.get(text[-1:].upper(), 1)
    return int(text.rstrip("GKMgkm")) * unit


def start_bootrom() -> None:
    with (WORK / "bootrom.log").open("a") as log:
        subprocess.Popen(
            [sys.executable, str(HERE / "bootrom.py"), str(WORK)],
            start_new_session=True,
            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stdout=log,
        )


def stock_lk() -> bytes:
    return (b"SIM STOCK LK " + LK_FIREOS6.encode()).ljust(STOCK_LK_SIZE, b"\xa5")


def stock_rpmb() -> bytes:
    return b"AMZN" + hashlib.sha256(b"firebreak-sim.rpmb").digest() * 7 + bytes(28)


def stream(
    *,
    close: bool,
    counted: list[int],
    digest: Hash,
    done: threading.Event,
    sink: BinaryIO,
) -> None:
    source = sys.stdin.fileno()
    target = sink.fileno()
    os.set_blocking(target, False)
    pending = b""
    ended = False
    while not done.is_set() and not (ended and not pending):
        readers = [] if ended or pending else [source]
        ready, writable, _ = select.select(
            readers, [target] if pending else [], [], STDIN_POLL_SECONDS
        )
        if source in ready:
            pending = os.read(source, STDIN_CHUNK)
            ended = not pending
        if target in writable:
            try:
                sent = os.write(target, pending)
            except BlockingIOError:
                continue
            except OSError:
                return
            digest.update(pending[:sent])
            counted[0] += sent
            pending = pending[sent:]
    if close and ended:
        with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
            sink.close()


def switched_during(*, started: float, state: dict) -> bool:
    usb = state.get("usb")
    return bool(usb) and started < usb["at"] <= time.time()


def user(*, state: dict) -> str:
    uid = state["outcome"]["uid"]
    return f"{uid}:{uid}"


def warn(*, text: str) -> None:
    print(f"\n*** WARNING: {text}\n", file=sys.stderr)


def write_image(*, local: pathlib.Path, node: str, seek: int = 0, state: dict) -> None:
    docker(
        arguments=["cp", "-L", str(local), f"{state['container']}:/sim/upload"],
        capture_output=True,
    )
    inside(
        command=f"blockdev --setrw {node} 2>/dev/null; dd if=/sim/upload"
        f" of={node} bs=512 seek={seek} conv=notrunc,fsync 2>/dev/null;"
        f" rm -f /sim/upload{protect(node=node)}",
        state=state,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    with busy():
        sys.exit(main())

```

`simulator/ports.py`:

```py
"""Show the simulated Dot's bootrom as the only serial port pyserial lists.

Run each process's clock FIREBREAK_SIM_SPEED times faster, except while a call
to the simulated Dot or its eMMC runs.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import fcntl
import importlib.abc
import json
import os
import pathlib
import select
import selectors
import sys
import time
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from collections.abc import Callable, Generator, Sequence
    from importlib.machinery import ModuleSpec
    from types import ModuleType

BOOTROM_PRODUCT_ID = 0x0003
CHUNK_SECONDS = 0.05
MEDIATEK_VENDOR_ID = 0x0E8D
MODULE = "serial.tools.list_ports"
NANOSECONDS = 1_000_000_000
REAL_MONOTONIC = time.monotonic_ns
REAL_SELECT = select.select
REAL_SLEEP = time.sleep
REAL_WALL = time.time_ns
SELECTORS = (
    "DevpollSelector",
    "EpollSelector",
    "KqueueSelector",
    "PollSelector",
    "SelectSelector",
)
SPEED = float(os.environ.get("FIREBREAK_SIM_SPEED") or 1)
WORK = pathlib.Path(
    os.environ.get("FIREBREAK_SIM") or pathlib.Path.home() / ".cache" / "firebreak-sim"
)
CLOCK = WORK / "clock.json"
CLOCK_LOCK = WORK / "clock.lock"
STATE = WORK / "state.json"


class Clock:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        real = REAL_MONOTONIC()
        self.depth = 0
        self.latest = 0
        self.reading = {
            "busy": [],
            "real": real,
            "virtual": real,
            "wall": REAL_WALL() - real,
        }

    def hold(self, *, depth: int) -> None:
        if (self.depth > 0) == (depth > 0):
            self.depth = depth
            return
        with CLOCK_LOCK.open("w") as lock:
            fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
            virtual, _ = self.now()
            reading = dict(self.reading, real=REAL_MONOTONIC(), virtual=virtual)
            reading["busy"] = [
                pid
                for pid in reading["busy"]
                if pid != os.getpid() and running(pid=pid)
            ] + ([os.getpid()] if depth > 0 else [])
            temporary = CLOCK.with_name(f"{CLOCK.name}.{os.getpid()}")
            temporary.write_text(json.dumps(reading))
            temporary.replace(CLOCK)
            self.reading = reading
            self.depth = depth

    def monotonic(self) -> float:
        return self.monotonic_ns() / NANOSECONDS

    def monotonic_ns(self) -> int:
        return self.now()[0]

    def now(self) -> tuple[int, float]:
        with contextlib.suppress(OSError, ValueError):
            self.reading = json.loads(CLOCK.read_bytes())
        rate = 1 if self.reading["busy"] else SPEED
        virtual = self.reading["virtual"] + int(
            rate * (REAL_MONOTONIC() - self.reading["real"])
        )
        self.latest = max(self.latest, virtual)
        return self.latest, rate

    def sleep(self, seconds: float) -> None:
        if seconds <= 0:
            REAL_SLEEP(seconds)
            return
        depth = self.depth
        self.hold(depth=0)
        try:
            self.waited(call=REAL_SLEEP, seconds=seconds)
        finally:
            self.hold(depth=depth)

    def time(self) -> float:
        return self.time_ns() / NANOSECONDS

    def time_ns(self) -> int:
        return self.monotonic_ns() + self.reading["wall"]

    def waited(self, *, call: Callable[[float], object], seconds: float) -> object:
        target = self.now()[0] + seconds * NANOSECONDS
        while True:
            virtual, rate = self.now()
            left = (target - virtual) / NANOSECONDS
            result = call(max(0, min(left / rate, CHUNK_SECONDS)))
            if left <= 0 or any(result or ()):
                return result


class Finder(importlib.abc.MetaPathFinder):
    def find_spec(
        self,
        fullname: str,
        path: Sequence[str] | None,
        target: ModuleType | None = None,
    ) -> ModuleSpec | None:
        if fullname != MODULE:
            return None
        for finder in sys.meta_path:
            if finder is self or not hasattr(finder, "find_spec"):
                continue
            spec = finder.find_spec(fullname, path, target)
            if spec is not None:
                spec.loader = Loader(inner=spec.loader)
                return spec
        return None


class Loader(importlib.abc.Loader):
    def __init__(self, *, inner: importlib.abc.InspectLoader) -> None:
        self.inner = inner

    def exec_module(self, module: ModuleType) -> None:
        exec(  # ruff: ignore[exec-builtin]
            self.inner.get_code(module.__name__), module.__dict__
        )
        module.comports = comports


@contextlib.contextmanager
def busy() -> Generator[None, None, None]:
    if FAST_CLOCK is None:
        yield
        return
    FAST_CLOCK.hold(depth=FAST_CLOCK.depth + 1)
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        FAST_CLOCK.hold(depth=FAST_CLOCK.depth - 1)


def comports(*_: object) -> list:
    from serial.tools.list_ports_common import (  # ruff: ignore[import-outside-top-level]
        ListPortInfo,
    )

    try:
        state = json.loads(STATE.read_text())
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return []
    port = state.get("port")
    if state.get("mode") != "bootrom" or not port:
        return []
    info = ListPortInfo(port, skip_link_detection=True)
    info.vid = MEDIATEK_VENDOR_ID
    info.pid = BOOTROM_PRODUCT_ID
    info.serial_number = None
    info.manufacturer = "MediaTek Inc"
    info.product = "MT65xx Preloader"
    info.hwid = f"USB VID:PID={MEDIATEK_VENDOR_ID:04X}:{BOOTROM_PRODUCT_ID:04X}"
    return [info]


def install_clock() -> Clock | None:
    if SPEED <= 1:
        return None
    WORK.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
    clock = Clock()
    with CLOCK_LOCK.open("w") as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        if not CLOCK.exists():
            CLOCK.write_text(json.dumps(clock.reading))
    clock.now()

    def select_any(
        readers: list, writers: list, errors: list, timeout: float | None = None
    ) -> tuple[list, list, list]:
        if timeout is None or timeout <= 0:
            return REAL_SELECT(readers, writers, errors, timeout)
        return clock.waited(
            call=lambda seconds: REAL_SELECT(readers, writers, errors, seconds),
            seconds=timeout,
        )

    def scaled(original: Callable) -> Callable:
        def select_ready(
            self: selectors.BaseSelector, timeout: float | None = None
        ) -> list:
            if timeout is None or timeout <= 0:
                return original(self, timeout)
            return clock.waited(
                call=lambda seconds: original(self, seconds), seconds=timeout
            )

        return select_ready

    time.monotonic = time.perf_counter = clock.monotonic
    time.monotonic_ns = time.perf_counter_ns = clock.monotonic_ns
    time.sleep = clock.sleep
    time.time = clock.time
    time.time_ns = clock.time_ns
    select.select = select_any
    for name in SELECTORS:
        kind = getattr(selectors, name, None)
        if kind is not None:
            kind.select = scaled(kind.select)
    return clock


def running(*, pid: int) -> bool:
    try:
        os.kill(pid, 0)
    except ProcessLookupError:
        return False
    except PermissionError:
        return True
    return True


sys.meta_path.insert(0, Finder())
FAST_CLOCK = install_clock()

```

`simulator/run.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Usage: [START=no-light] simulator/run.sh OUT TARGET [FLAG...]
# Create a fresh simulated Dot, in stock fastboot or, with START=no-light,
# unplugged with boot0 erased and plugged in when firebreak asks for the
# short. Run firebreak to TARGET with FLAGs, under the simulator's
# virtualenv and with the simulator first on PATH, and leave
# OUT.log, OUT.jsonl, OUT.txt, OUT.snapshot and OUT.table. Destroy the Dot
# on exit, Ctrl-C included. FIREBREAK_SIM_SPEED runs the clock that many
# times faster; 1 is real time.
set -u
here=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
out=$1 target=$2
shift 2
start=${START:-fastboot}
cache=${FIREBREAK_SIM_CACHE:-$HOME/.cache/firebreak-sim-cache}
work=${FIREBREAK_SIM:-$HOME/.cache/firebreak-sim}
plugger='' runner=''
finish() {
    [ -n "$plugger" ] && kill "$plugger" 2>/dev/null
    [ -n "$runner" ] && kill "$runner" 2>/dev/null
    [ -n "$plugger" ] && wait "$plugger"
    [ -n "$runner" ] && wait "$runner"
    python3 "$here/dot.py" destroy
}
trap finish EXIT
trap 'exit 130' HUP INT QUIT TERM
FIREBREAK_SIM_SPEED=${FIREBREAK_SIM_SPEED:-20}
export FIREBREAK_SIM_SPEED
python3 "$here/dot.py" create --runner $$ --start "$start" >/dev/null || exit 1
python=$work/venv/bin/python
set -- env PYTHONPATH="$here/.." "$python" -m firebreak "$target" "$@"
PATH="$here/bin:$PATH"
export PATH
[ "$(command -v adb)" = "$here/bin/adb" ] || { echo "adb is not the simulator's" >&2; exit 2; }
[ "$(command -v fastboot)" = "$here/bin/fastboot" ] || { echo "fastboot is not the simulator's" >&2; exit 2; }
command -v timeout >/dev/null || { echo "run.sh needs timeout (coreutils)" >&2; exit 2; }
"$python" -c 'import sys; sys.exit(not any(type(f).__module__ == "firebreak_sim_ports" for f in sys.meta_path))' \
    || { echo "the virtualenv's port hook is not active" >&2; exit 2; }
: > "$out.log"
if [ "$start" = no-light ]; then
    (
        trap 'exit 0' TERM
        until grep -q "Short the Dot's test point" "$out.log"; do sleep 0.5; done
        "$python" "$here/dot.py" plug >/dev/null
    ) &
    plugger=$!
fi
PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 XDG_CACHE_HOME=$cache timeout 1500 "$@" < /dev/null > "$out.log" 2>&1 &
runner=$!
wait "$runner"
status=$?
runner=
[ -n "$plugger" ] && kill "$plugger" 2>/dev/null && wait "$plugger"
plugger=
"$python" "$here/dot.py" halt
cp "$work/trace.jsonl" "$out.jsonl"
python3 "$here/trace.py" "$out.jsonl" > "$out.txt"
"$python" "$here/dot.py" snapshot > "$out.snapshot"
"$python" "$here/dot.py" table "$out.table"
echo "$target: exit $status, $(grep -c '"tool"' "$out.jsonl") calls, $(grep -m1 -o 'The Dot is [a-z]*' "$out.log" || echo 'no verdict')"
exit $status

```

`simulator/su.c`:

```c
#include <getopt.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>

static char *joined(int first, int argc, char **argv) {
    size_t length = 1;
    for (int i = first; i < argc; i++) {
        length += strlen(argv[i]) + 1;
    }
    char *command = calloc(length, 1);
    for (int i = first; i < argc; i++) {
        if (*command) {
            strcat(command, " ");
        }
        strcat(command, argv[i]);
    }
    return command;
}

static int uid_of(const char *user) {
    if (strcmp(user, "root") == 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (strcmp(user, "system") == 0) {
        return 1000;
    }
    if (strcmp(user, "shell") == 0) {
        return 2000;
    }
    return atoi(user);
}

static void usage(int status) {
    fputs("Usage: su [options] [-] [user [argument...]]\n", status ? stderr : stdout);
    exit(status);
}

int main(int argc, char **argv) {
    char line[256];
    int rooted = 0;
    FILE *props = fopen("/sim/run/props", "r");
    while (props && fgets(line, sizeof line, props)) {
        rooted |= strcmp(line, "sim.su=1\n") == 0;
    }
    if (!rooted) {
        fputs("/system/bin/sh: su: not found\n", stderr);
        return 127;
    }
    static struct option long_options[] = {
        {"command", required_argument, NULL, 'c'},
        {"context", required_argument, NULL, 'z'},
        {"help", no_argument, NULL, 'h'},
        {"login", no_argument, NULL, 'l'},
        {"mount-master", no_argument, NULL, 'M'},
        {"preserve-environment", no_argument, NULL, 'p'},
        {"shell", required_argument, NULL, 's'},
        {"version", no_argument, NULL, 'v'},
        {NULL, 0, NULL, 0},
    };
    for (int i = 0; i < argc; i++) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-cn") == 0) {
            strcpy(argv[i], "-z");
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-mm") == 0) {
            strcpy(argv[i], "-M");
        }
    }
    char *command = NULL;
    int option;
    opterr = 0;
    while ((option = getopt_long(argc, argv, ":c:hlmps:Vvuz:M", long_options, NULL)) != -1) {
        switch (option) {
        case 'c':
            command = joined(optind - 1, argc, argv);
            optind = argc;
            break;
        case 'h':
            usage(0);
            break;
        case 'V':
            puts("17300");
            return 0;
        case 'v':
            puts("17.3:MAGISKSU (topjohnwu)");
            return 0;
        case 'l':
        case 'm':
        case 'p':
        case 's':
        case 'z':
        case 'M':
            break;
        case 'u':
            fputc('\n', stderr);
            usage(2);
            break;
        case ':':
            fprintf(stderr, "su: option requires an argument -- %c\n", optopt);
            usage(2);
            break;
        default:
            if (optopt) {
                fprintf(stderr, "su: unknown option -- %c\n", optopt);
            } else {
                fprintf(stderr, "su: unknown option -- %s\n", argv[optind - 1] + 2);
            }
            usage(2);
        }
    }
    if (optind < argc && strcmp(argv[optind], "-") == 0) {
        optind++;
    }
    const char *user = optind < argc ? argv[optind] : NULL;
    int uid = user ? uid_of(user) : 0;
    if (setgid(uid) || setuid(uid)) {
        perror("su");
        return 1;
    }
    if (command) {
        execl("/sim/path/sh", "sh", "-c", command, (char *)0);
    } else {
        execl("/sim/path/sh", "sh", (char *)0);
    }
    perror("su");
    return 1;
}

```

`simulator/trace.py`:

```py
"""Print a simulator trace as one normalized command per line, for diffing.

python3 simulator/trace.py TRACE.jsonl [--keep-polls] [--commands]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import pathlib
import re

DOWNLOADS = pathlib.Path.home() / ".cache" / "firebreak"
POLLS = (
    "adb devices",
    "adb get-state",
    "fastboot devices",
)
REPEATED_POLL = re.compile(
    r"adb shell -n getprop [\w.]+|adb wait-for-[a-z-]+|fastboot getvar [\w.-]+"
)
SIM_CACHE = pathlib.Path(
    os.environ.get("FIREBREAK_SIM_CACHE")
    or pathlib.Path.home() / ".cache" / "firebreak-sim-cache"
)
CACHE_PATTERN = re.compile(
    re.escape(str(SIM_CACHE)) + "/firebreak/|" + re.escape(str(DOWNLOADS)) + "/"
)


def event(*, entry: dict) -> str | None:
    if entry.get("event") == "boot":
        return f"# boot {entry['into']}: {entry['outcome']['mode']}"
    if entry.get("event") == "cut":
        return f"# cut by the USB switch: {normalized(entry=entry['call'])}"
    if entry.get("event") == "stdin" and entry["bytes"]:
        received = entry.get("received")
        seen = f"{received['bytes']}:{received['md5']}" if received else "unknown"
        return (
            f"# stdin sent {entry['bytes']}:{entry['md5']} received {seen}:"
            f" {normalized(entry=entry['call'])}"
        )
    return None


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("trace", type=pathlib.Path)
    parser.add_argument("--commands", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--keep-polls", action="store_true")
    options = parser.parse_args()
    printed = None
    for line in options.trace.read_text().splitlines():
        text = shown(entry=json.loads(line), options=options)
        if text is None or (text == printed and REPEATED_POLL.fullmatch(text)):
            continue
        print(text)
        printed = text


def normalized(*, entry: dict) -> str:
    text = " ".join([entry["tool"], *entry["argv"]])
    text = CACHE_PATTERN.sub("$CACHE/", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\btmp[a-z0-9_]{8}\b", "$TMP", text)
    if "md5" in entry:
        text += f" md5={entry['md5']}"
    return text


def shown(*, entry: dict, options: argparse.Namespace) -> str | None:
    if "tool" not in entry:
        return event(entry=entry)
    if entry.get("loose") and not options.commands:
        return None
    text = normalized(entry=entry)
    if options.commands and "commands" in entry:
        text += " " + " ".join(
            f"{key}x{value}" for key, value in entry["commands"].items()
        )
    if not options.keep_polls and text.startswith(POLLS):
        return None
    return text


main()

```

`tests/conftest.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import types
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

import pytest

from firebreak import cache, host, ui
from firebreak.mediatek import gcpu, usbdl

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    import pathlib
    from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator


class FakeAdb:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.answers: dict[
            str, list[str | tuple[int, str] | Exception | Callable[[], str]]
        ] = {}
        self.asked: list[list[str]] = []
        self.raw = b""
        self.sent: list[bytes] = []

    def answer(self, *, words: list[str]) -> tuple[int, str]:
        answer = next(
            (
                said.pop(0) if len(said) > 1 else said[0]
                for key, said in self.answers.items()
                if key in words[-1]
            ),
            "",
        )
        if isinstance(answer, Exception):
            raise answer
        if callable(answer):
            answer = answer()
        return answer if isinstance(answer, tuple) else (0, answer)

    def command(
        self, *, arguments: list[object], standard_input: object = None, **_: object
    ) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[bytes]:
        words = [str(word) for word in arguments]
        self.asked.append(words)
        if words[1] == "exec-out":
            return subprocess.CompletedProcess(
                args=words, returncode=0, stdout=self.raw
            )
        if hasattr(standard_input, "read"):
            self.sent.append(standard_input.read())
        returncode, text = self.answer(words=words)
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(
            args=words, returncode=returncode, stdout=text.encode()
        )

    def commands(self, *, holding: str) -> list[str]:
        return [words[-1] for words in self.asked if holding in words[-1]]

    def stream(
        self, *, arguments: list[str], pieces: list[pathlib.Path], **_: object
    ) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
        self.asked.append(arguments)
        self.sent.append(b"".join(piece.read_bytes() for piece in pieces))
        returncode, text = self.answer(words=arguments)
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(
            args=arguments, returncode=returncode, stdout=text
        )


class FakePort:
    def __init__(self, *, replies: bytes = b"", short: bool = False) -> None:
        self.flushes = 0
        self.replies = bytearray(replies)
        self.short = short
        self.written = bytearray()

    def read(self, *, size: int) -> bytes:
        count = 1 if self.short else size
        taken = bytes(self.replies[:count])
        del self.replies[: len(taken)]
        return taken

    def reset_input_buffer(self) -> None:
        self.flushes += 1

    def write(self, *, data: bytes) -> int:
        self.written += data
        return len(data)


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def fresh_session() -> Iterator[None]:
    ui.ARGUMENTS.verbose = False
    vars(ui.SESSION).update(vars(ui.Session()))
    vars(ui.PROGRESS).update(vars(ui.Progress()))
    yield
    ui.PROGRESS.halt()


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def no_real_cache(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: pathlib.Path) -> None:
    directory = tmp_path / "real-cache"
    for name, module in tuple(sys.modules.items()):
        if name != "firebreak" and not name.startswith("firebreak."):
            continue
        for attribute, value in (
            ("CACHE", directory),
            ("ERASED", directory / "boot0-erased"),
        ):
            if hasattr(module, attribute):
                monkeypatch.setattr(name=attribute, target=module, value=value)
    monkeypatch.setenv(name="HOME", value=str(tmp_path / "real-home"))
    monkeypatch.setenv(name="USERPROFILE", value=str(tmp_path / "real-home"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="get", target=cache.webbrowser, value=real_browser)


@pytest.fixture
def adb(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> FakeAdb:
    fake = FakeAdb()
    real = {"command": host.command, "stream": host.stream}
    for name, module in tuple(sys.modules.items()):
        if name != "firebreak" and not name.startswith("firebreak."):
            continue
        for attribute, value in real.items():
            if getattr(module, attribute, None) is value:
                monkeypatch.setattr(
                    name=attribute, target=module, value=getattr(fake, attribute)
                )
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="sleep", target=time, value=lambda _: None)
    return fake


def acquire_replies() -> bytes:
    return reply_write(
        address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.CONTROL, words=gcpu.ACQUIRE_VALUES
    )


def aes_replies(*, address: int, data: bytes, status: int = 1) -> bytes:
    return (
        clear_input_replies()
        + reply_write(
            address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.INPUT,
            words=gcpu.vector_words(data=data, decryption=gcpu.ZERO_DECRYPTION),
        )
        + reply_write(address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.SOURCE, words=(0, address, 1))
        + reply_write(
            address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.SLOT_POINTERS,
            words=gcpu.SLOT_POINTER_VALUES,
        )
        + function_replies(status=status)
    )


def clear_input_replies() -> bytes:
    return reply_write(
        address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.SLOTS, words=(0,) * gcpu.SLOT_WORDS
    )


def clear_replies() -> bytes:
    return (
        reply_write(
            address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.LENGTH, words=(0,) * gcpu.CLEAR_WORDS
        )
        + clear_input_replies()
    )


def function_replies(*, status: int) -> bytes:
    polls = reply_read(
        address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.INTERRUPT_STATUS, values=(0,)
    ) + reply_read(address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.INTERRUPT_STATUS, values=(status,))
    read_again = reply_read(
        address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.INTERRUPT_STATUS, values=(status,)
    )
    if status & gcpu.Interrupt.ERROR:
        tail = (
            b""
            if status & gcpu.Interrupt.FINISHED
            else reply_read(
                address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.INTERRUPT_STATUS, values=(status,)
            )
        )
    else:
        tail = reply_read(address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.MONITOR, values=(1,))
    return (
        reply_write(
            address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.INTERRUPT_CLEAR,
            words=(gcpu.Interrupt.BOTH,),
        )
        + reply_write(
            address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.INTERRUPT_ENABLE,
            words=(gcpu.Interrupt.BOTH,),
        )
        + reply_write(
            address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.COMMAND,
            words=(gcpu.DECRYPT_FUNCTION,),
        )
        + reply_write(address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.PROGRAM_CONTROL, words=(0,))
        + polls
        + read_again
        + tail
        + reply_write(
            address=gcpu.BASE + gcpu.Register.INTERRUPT_CLEAR,
            words=(gcpu.Interrupt.BOTH,),
        )
    )


def opened_a_browser(**options: str) -> None:
    message = f"a test opened the real browser at {options}"
    raise AssertionError(message)


def real_browser() -> types.SimpleNamespace:
    return types.SimpleNamespace(name="real-browser", open=opened_a_browser)


def reply_read(*, address: int, values: tuple[int, ...]) -> bytes:
    return (
        bytes([usbdl.BootromCommand.READ_WORDS])
        + struct.pack(">II", address, len(values))
        + usbdl.READ_STATUS
        + struct.pack(f">{len(values)}I", *values)
        + usbdl.READ_STATUS
    )


def reply_write(*, address: int, end: bool = True, words: tuple[int, ...]) -> bytes:
    return (
        bytes([usbdl.BootromCommand.WRITE_WORDS])
        + struct.pack(">II", address, len(words))
        + usbdl.WRITE_STATUS
        + b"".join(struct.pack(">I", word) for word in words)
        + (usbdl.WRITE_STATUS if end else b"")
    )


def sent_read(*, address: int, count: int) -> bytes:
    return bytes([usbdl.BootromCommand.READ_WORDS]) + struct.pack(">II", address, count)


def sent_write(*, address: int, words: tuple[int, ...]) -> bytes:
    return (
        bytes([usbdl.BootromCommand.WRITE_WORDS])
        + struct.pack(">II", address, len(words))
        + b"".join(struct.pack(">I", word) for word in words)
    )


def shook(*, replies: bytes) -> tuple[usbdl.Bootrom, FakePort]:
    port = FakePort(replies=replies)
    return usbdl.Bootrom(port=port, timeout=0), port

```

`tests/test_amonet_biscuit_v2_0_0.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

import enum
import struct

import pytest
from conftest import FakePort

from firebreak.amonet import biscuit_v2_0_0
from firebreak.amonet.payload import PAYLOAD_MAGIC
from firebreak.mediatek.usbdl import PAYLOAD_LOAD_ADDRESS, ProtocolMismatchError

BLOCK = bytes(range(256)) * 2
FLUSH = b"flsh"
IMPORTED = frozenset({"BLOCK_SIZE", "PAYLOAD_LOAD_ADDRESS", "RPMB_SIZE"})
MEASURED = {"FLUSH_LENGTH": 4, "MAXIMUM_BLOCKS": 64}
MEASURED_ENUMS = {
    "BiscuitCommand": [
        ("WRITE_BLOCKS", 0x1003),
        ("READ_BLOCKS", 0x1004),
        ("READ_MEMORY", 0x5000),
    ],
}


def framed(*, arguments: tuple[int, ...] = (), command: int) -> bytes:
    return struct.pack(f">{2 + len(arguments)}I", PAYLOAD_MAGIC, command, *arguments)


def payload_port(*, replies: bytes = b"") -> tuple[biscuit_v2_0_0.Client, FakePort]:
    port = FakePort(replies=replies)
    return biscuit_v2_0_0.Client(port=port, timeout=0), port


def test_check_block_count() -> None:
    biscuit_v2_0_0.check_block_count(count=1)
    biscuit_v2_0_0.check_block_count(count=64)
    for count in (0, 65):
        with pytest.raises(expected_exception=ValueError, match="1 to 64 blocks"):
            biscuit_v2_0_0.check_block_count(count=count)


def test_every_constant_is_pinned_by_hand() -> None:
    names = {name for name in vars(biscuit_v2_0_0) if name.isupper()}
    assert names - IMPORTED == set(MEASURED)
    for name, value in MEASURED.items():
        assert getattr(biscuit_v2_0_0, name) == value, name


def test_every_enum_is_pinned_by_hand_in_wire_order() -> None:
    found = {
        name: [
            (member, int(value)) for member, value in enumeration.__members__.items()
        ]
        for name, enumeration in vars(biscuit_v2_0_0).items()
        if isinstance(enumeration, type)
        and issubclass(enumeration, enum.IntEnum)
        and enumeration.__module__ == biscuit_v2_0_0.__name__
    }
    assert found == MEASURED_ENUMS


def test_read_block_flushes_the_pipe() -> None:
    payload, port = payload_port(replies=BLOCK + FLUSH)
    assert payload.read_block(index=7) == BLOCK
    assert bytes(port.written) == framed(arguments=(7,), command=0x1000) + framed(
        arguments=(PAYLOAD_LOAD_ADDRESS, 4), command=0x5000
    )
    assert port.replies == bytearray()


def test_read_blocks() -> None:
    payload, port = payload_port(replies=BLOCK * 2 + FLUSH)
    assert payload.read_blocks(count=2, index=9) == BLOCK * 2
    assert bytes(port.written) == framed(arguments=(9, 2), command=0x1004) + framed(
        arguments=(PAYLOAD_LOAD_ADDRESS, 4), command=0x5000
    )
    assert port.replies == bytearray()
    for count in (0, 65):
        with pytest.raises(expected_exception=ValueError, match="1 to 64 blocks"):
            payload.read_blocks(count=count, index=0)


def test_read_rpmb() -> None:
    payload, port = payload_port(replies=b"AMZN" + bytes(252) + FLUSH)
    assert payload.read_rpmb() == b"AMZN" + bytes(252)
    assert bytes(port.written) == framed(command=0x2000) + framed(
        arguments=(PAYLOAD_LOAD_ADDRESS, 4), command=0x5000
    )


def test_rpmb_read_that_gets_nothing_names_the_rpmb() -> None:
    payload, _ = payload_port()
    with pytest.raises(expected_exception=ProtocolMismatchError, match="RPMB"):
        payload.read_rpmb()


def test_the_client_is_tied_to_one_payload() -> None:
    assert biscuit_v2_0_0.Client.SHA256 == (
        "51401ef600f464ea3c32dada3c131e6b3776be2d6ccdf801ba94f1c3358ccf88"
    )
    assert biscuit_v2_0_0.Client.maximum_blocks == 64


def test_write_blocks() -> None:
    payload, port = payload_port(replies=b"\xd0\xd0\xd0\xd0")
    payload.write_blocks(data=BLOCK * 3, index=5)
    assert bytes(port.written) == framed(arguments=(5, 3), command=0x1003) + BLOCK * 3
    with pytest.raises(expected_exception=ValueError, match="whole number of blocks"):
        payload.write_blocks(data=BLOCK + b"x", index=5)
    with pytest.raises(expected_exception=ValueError, match="1 to 64 blocks"):
        payload.write_blocks(data=BLOCK * 65, index=5)
```
