`tests/test_stock.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import pathlib
import subprocess
import types
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

import pytest

from firebreak import stock
from firebreak.android.gpt import Partition
from firebreak.ui import FatalError

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from conftest import FakeAdb

BOOT0_SECTORS = 8
PARTITIONS = {
    "boot_a": Partition(first=100, number=1, sectors=64),
    "boot_b": Partition(first=200, number=2, sectors=64),
    "cache": Partition(first=300, number=3, sectors=64),
    "expdb": Partition(first=400, number=4, sectors=8),
    "lk_a": Partition(first=500, number=5, sectors=8),
    "lk_b": Partition(first=600, number=6, sectors=8),
    "misc": Partition(first=700, number=7, sectors=4),
    "system_a": Partition(first=800, number=8, sectors=64),
    "system_b": Partition(first=900, number=9, sectors=64),
    "tee1": Partition(first=1000, number=10, sectors=8),
    "tee2": Partition(first=1100, number=11, sectors=8),
    "userdata": Partition(first=1200, number=12, sectors=4096),
}
PRELOADER = b"P" * (BOOT0_SECTORS * 512)
RAW = b"M" * 512 + b"H" * 512 + b"E" * 16384


@pytest.fixture
def restored(*, adb: FakeAdb, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> FakeAdb:
    adb.answers["sgdisk --verify"] = ["No problems found"]
    adb.answers["grep -c"] = ["0"]
    adb.answers[f" {PARTITIONS['userdata'].size // 1024} mmcblk0p12$"] = ["12"]
    adb.answers["echo formatted"] = ["formatted"]
    adb.answers["slot_suffix"] = ["_a"]
    adb.answers["md5sum /dev/block/mmcblk0boot0"] = [md5(data=PRELOADER) + "  boot0"]
    stock.ERASED.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="clear_boot0", target=stock, value=lambda: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="unmount", target=stock, value=lambda **_: None)
    return adb


@pytest.fixture
def twrp(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
    monkeypatch.setenv(name="ANDROID_SERIAL", value="G000")
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="build", target=stock.ARGUMENTS, value="8146")
    adb.answers["sys.usb.config"] = ["mtp,adb"]
    adb.answers["ro.twrp.version"] = ["3.7.0_9-0"]
    adb.answers["toybox dd --help"] = ["yes"]
    adb.answers["for t in sgdisk"] = ["tools:"]
    adb.answers["ro.product.device"] = ["biscuit"]
    adb.answers["echo block"] = ["block"]
    adb.answers["mmcblk0boot0/size"] = [str(BOOT0_SECTORS)]
    adb.answers["slot_suffix"] = ["_a"]

    def extract(*, update: pathlib.Path, work: pathlib.Path) -> None:
        del update
        for name, size in (
            ("boot.img", 1024),
            ("lk.img", 1024),
            ("system.img", 4096),
            ("tee.img", 1024),
        ):
            (work / name).write_bytes(b"x" * size)
        (work / "preloader.img").write_bytes(PRELOADER)

    restores: list[dict[str, object]] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="download", target=stock.updates, value=lambda **_: tmp_path / "ota.bin"
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="extract", target=stock, value=extract)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="gpt_intact", target=stock, value=lambda **_: True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="read_sectors", target=stock, value=lambda **_: RAW)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="stock_gpt",
        target=stock,
        value=lambda **_: types.SimpleNamespace(
            backup=b"B" * 512,
            backup_sector=9999,
            partitions=PARTITIONS,
            primary=b"A" * 512,
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="restore", target=stock, value=lambda **options: restores.append(options)
    )
    return restores


def md5(*, data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.md5(data, usedforsecurity=False).hexdigest()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("key", "answer", "said"),
    [
        ("sgdisk --verify", "Problem: bad", "sgdisk does not accept the new table"),
        ("grep -c", "2", "still sees amonet's partitions"),
        (f" {PARTITIONS['userdata'].size // 1024} mmcblk0p12$", "", "not its stock"),
        ("echo formatted", "mke2fs: failed", "did not format"),
        ("pl.img", (1, "adb: error"), "the preloader did not reach the Dot"),
        (
            "md5sum /dev/block/mmcblk0boot0",
            "0" * 32 + "  boot0",
            "boot0 does not match the 8146 preloader: read 000",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_a_restore_that_goes_wrong_says_do_not_reboot(  # ruff: ignore[too-many-arguments]
    *,
    answer: str | tuple[int, str],
    key: str,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    restored: FakeAdb,
    said: str,
    tmp_path: pathlib.Path,
) -> None:
    wrote(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    restored.answers[key] = [answer]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match=said) as stopped:
        stock.restore(
            backup_sector=9999,
            build="8146",
            files=written(tmp_path=tmp_path),
            parts=PARTITIONS,
            work=tmp_path,
        )
    assert "do not reboot" in stopped.value.message
    assert stock.ERASED.exists()


def test_a_restore_that_times_out_rebooting_still_finishes(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, restored: FakeAdb, tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    wrote(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    restored.answers["reboot"] = [subprocess.TimeoutExpired(cmd="adb", timeout=60)]
    stock.restore(
        backup_sector=9999,
        build="8146",
        files=written(tmp_path=tmp_path),
        parts=PARTITIONS,
        work=tmp_path,
    )
    assert stock.PROGRESS.open


def test_a_restore_writes_in_order_and_starts_stock(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, restored: FakeAdb, tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    writes = wrote(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    stock.restore(
        backup_sector=9999,
        build="8146",
        files=written(tmp_path=tmp_path),
        parts=PARTITIONS,
        work=tmp_path,
    )
    assert writes == [
        ("write the system image to system_a", 8, "system", 800),
        ("write the system image to system_b", 9, "system", 900),
        ("write boot image to boot_a", 1, "boot", 100),
        ("write boot image to boot_b", 2, "boot", 200),
        ("write misc, slot a marked good", 7, "misc", 700),
        ("write the stock table (backup)", None, "gpt-backup.bin", 9999),
        ("write the stock table (primary)", None, "gpt-primary.bin", 0),
        ("write LK image to lk_b (spare slot)", 6, "lk", 600),
        ("write TEE image to tee2 (backup)", 11, "tee", 1100),
        ("zero expdb (amonet kaeru payload)", 4, "expdb", 400),
        ("write LK image to lk_a (live slot)", 5, "lk", 500),
        ("write TEE image to tee1 (primary)", 10, "tee", 1000),
    ]
    assert not stock.ERASED.exists()
    assert restored.commands(holding="if=/tmp/pl.img of=/dev/block/mmcblk0boot0 ")
    assert restored.asked[-1][-1] == "reboot"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("raised", "finished", "said"),
    [
        (
            subprocess.TimeoutExpired(cmd=["adb", "push"], timeout=120),
            False,
            "adb push did not finish in 120 seconds. Do not reboot",
        ),
        (KeyboardInterrupt(), False, "stopped part way. Do not reboot"),
        (KeyboardInterrupt(), True, "stopped; stock 8146 is in place"),
    ],
)
def test_a_stock_stage_that_stops_part_way_says_whether_to_reboot(
    *,
    finished: bool,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    raised: BaseException,
    said: str,
    twrp: list[dict[str, object]],
) -> None:
    del twrp

    def restore(**_: object) -> None:
        stock.PROGRESS.steps = 16
        stock.PROGRESS.step = 16 if finished else 3
        raise raised

    monkeypatch.setattr(name="restore", target=stock, value=restore)
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match=said):
        stock.stage_restore()


def test_chain_writes_put_the_live_lk_slot_after_the_spare(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.answers["slot_suffix"] = ["_b"]
    assert [step.partition for step in stock.chain_writes()] == [
        "lk_a",
        "tee2",
        "expdb",
        "lk_b",
        "tee1",
    ]
    adb.answers["slot_suffix"] = ["_a"]
    labels = [step.label for step in stock.chain_writes()]
    assert labels[0] == "write LK image to lk_b (spare slot)"
    assert labels[3] == "write LK image to lk_a (live slot)"


def test_the_stock_stage_builds_its_images_from_the_dot(
    *,
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    tmp_path: pathlib.Path,
    twrp: list[dict[str, object]],
) -> None:
    del tmp_path
    stock.stage_restore()
    work = stock.CACHE / "stock-8146"
    assert len(twrp) == 1
    options = twrp[0]
    assert options["backup_sector"] == 9999
    assert options["build"] == "8146"
    files = options["files"]
    assert files["gpt-primary.bin"].read_bytes() == b"A" * 512
    assert files["gpt-backup.bin"].read_bytes() == b"B" * 512
    assert files["boot"].stat().st_size == PARTITIONS["boot_a"].size
    assert files["expdb"].read_bytes() == bytes(PARTITIONS["expdb"].size)
    misc = files["misc"].read_bytes()
    assert misc[0x360:0x367] == b"\0ABB\x01\x8f\0"
    assert len(misc) == PARTITIONS["misc"].size
    assert (work / "current-gpt-G000.bin").read_bytes() == RAW
    assert (work / "boot0.img").read_bytes() == PRELOADER
    assert stock.SESSION.dd == "toybox dd"
    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert "stock partition table built from this Dot's own" in out
    assert "Starting now." in out


def test_the_stock_stage_can_be_cancelled_in_its_countdown(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, twrp: list[dict[str, object]]
) -> None:
    slept: list[float] = []

    def interrupt(seconds: float) -> None:
        slept.append(seconds)
        if len(slept) > 1:
            raise KeyboardInterrupt

    monkeypatch.setattr(name="sleep", target=stock.time, value=interrupt)
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="stopped; nothing was written"):
        stock.stage_restore()
    assert twrp == []


def test_the_stock_stage_gives_up_on_a_twrp_that_never_starts(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, twrp: list[dict[str, object]]
) -> None:
    clock = iter(range(0, 1000, 10))
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="monotonic", target=stock.time, value=lambda: next(clock))
    adb.answers["ro.twrp.version"] = [""]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="within 30 seconds"):
        stock.stage_restore()
    assert twrp == []


def test_the_stock_stage_keeps_the_first_table_it_saved(
    *, twrp: list[dict[str, object]]
) -> None:
    saved = stock.CACHE / "stock-8146" / "current-gpt-G000.bin"
    saved.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    saved.write_bytes(b"first")
    stock.stage_restore()
    assert saved.read_bytes() == b"first"
    assert twrp


def test_the_stock_stage_pads_the_preloader_to_a_larger_boot0(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, tmp_path: pathlib.Path, twrp: list[dict[str, object]]
) -> None:
    del tmp_path
    adb.answers["mmcblk0boot0/size"] = [str(4 * BOOT0_SECTORS)]
    stock.stage_restore()
    boot0 = (stock.CACHE / "stock-8146" / "boot0.img").read_bytes()
    assert boot0 == PRELOADER + bytes(3 * len(PRELOADER))
    assert len(twrp) == 1


def test_the_stock_stage_reads_the_backup_table_when_the_primary_is_damaged(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, twrp: list[dict[str, object]]
) -> None:
    reads: list[dict[str, int]] = []
    tail = b"T" * (32 * 512) + b"h" * 512

    def read_sectors(**options: int) -> bytes:
        reads.append(options)
        return RAW if options["start"] == 0 else tail

    monkeypatch.setattr(name="gpt_intact", target=stock, value=lambda **_: False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="read_sectors", target=stock, value=read_sectors)
    adb.answers["blockdev --getsize64"] = [str(1000 * 512)]
    stock.stage_restore()
    assert reads[1] == {"count": 33, "start": 1000 - 33}
    saved = stock.CACHE / "stock-8146" / "current-gpt-G000.bin"
    assert saved.read_bytes() == RAW[:512] + b"h" * 512 + b"T" * (32 * 512)
    assert twrp


def test_the_stock_stage_refuses_an_image_too_big_for_its_partition(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, twrp: list[dict[str, object]]
) -> None:
    small = dict(PARTITIONS, tee1=Partition(first=1000, number=10, sectors=1))
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="stock_gpt",
        target=stock,
        value=lambda **_: types.SimpleNamespace(
            backup=b"", backup_sector=1, partitions=small, primary=b""
        ),
    )
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match=r"tee\.img does not fit tee1"):
        stock.stage_restore()
    assert twrp == []


def test_the_stock_stage_waits_for_twrp_to_finish_starting(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, twrp: list[dict[str, object]]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["ro.twrp.version"] = ["", "3.2.3-0"]
    adb.answers["sys.usb.config"] = ["", "mtp"]
    stock.stage_restore()
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="ro.twrp.version")) == 3
    assert twrp


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("key", "answer", "said"),
    [
        ("ro.twrp.version", "2.8.7.0", "this needs a TWRP for this Dot"),
        ("for t in sgdisk", "nothing", "did not answer which tools it has: nothing"),
        ("for t in sgdisk", "tools: sgdisk", "this TWRP has no sgdisk"),
        ("ro.product.device", "crown", "TWRP reports the device 'crown'"),
        ("echo block", "", "is not a block device"),
        ("mmcblk0boot0/size", "4", r"preloader\.img does not fit boot0 \(4 sectors\)"),
        ("blockdev --getsize64", "", "could not read the Dot's disk size"),
    ],
)
def test_the_stock_stage_writes_nothing_to_a_dot_it_cannot_check(  # ruff: ignore[too-many-arguments]
    *,
    adb: FakeAdb,
    answer: str,
    key: str,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    said: str,
    twrp: list[dict[str, object]],
) -> None:
    if key == "blockdev --getsize64":
        monkeypatch.setattr(name="gpt_intact", target=stock, value=lambda **_: False)
    adb.answers[key] = [answer]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match=said):
        stock.stage_restore()
    assert twrp == []


def written(*, tmp_path: pathlib.Path) -> dict[str, pathlib.Path]:
    files = {}
    for name in ("boot", "expdb", "gpt-backup.bin", "gpt-primary.bin", "lk"):
        files[name] = tmp_path / name
    for name in ("misc", "system", "tee"):
        files[name] = tmp_path / name
    (tmp_path / "boot0.img").write_bytes(PRELOADER)
    return files


def wrote(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> list[tuple[str, object, str, object]]:
    writes: list[tuple[str, object, str, object]] = []

    def write(**options: object) -> None:
        path = options["path"]
        assert isinstance(path, pathlib.Path)
        writes.append((
            str(options["label"]),
            options.get("number"),
            path.name,
            options["sector"],
        ))

    monkeypatch.setattr(name="write", target=stock, value=write)
    return writes

```

`tests/test_support.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import types
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

import pytest

from firebreak import support

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from collections.abc import Callable

    from conftest import FakeAdb

LISTING = (
    "lrwxrwxrwx root root 2026-10-11 00:00 cache -> /dev/block/mmcblk0p3\n"
    f"{support.LISTING_OK}"
)
PARTITIONS = (
    f"major minor  #blocks  name\n 179  3  65536 mmcblk0p3\n{support.PARTITIONS_OK}"
)


class Dot:
    @property
    def place(self) -> str:
        return self.places[0]

    def __init__(self, *, places: list[str]) -> None:
        self.places = places
        self.ran: list[list[str]] = []
        self.said: dict[str, str] = {
            "getprop ro.twrp.version": "3.7.0_9-0",
            "getprop sys.usb.config": "mtp,adb",
            "id; su -c id": "uid=0(root)",
        }
        self.variables = {
            "lk_build_desc": "f379dba-20170906_000423",
            "product": "BISCUIT",
            "unlock_status": "true",
        }

    def adb_shell(self, *, command: str, timeout: float) -> str:
        del timeout
        if command == "getprop sys.usb.config" and len(self.places) > 1:
            self.places.pop(0)
            return ""
        return self.said.get(command, "")

    def getvar(self, *, name: str) -> str:
        return self.variables[name]

    def in_fastboot(self) -> bool:
        return self.place == "fastboot"

    def run(self, *, arguments: list[str], timeout: float) -> types.SimpleNamespace:
        del timeout
        self.ran.append(arguments)
        if arguments[-1] == "wait-for-recovery":
            if "lost" in self.places:
                raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired(cmd=arguments, timeout=180)
            self.places.pop(0)
        if arguments[-1] == "get-state":
            return types.SimpleNamespace(stdout=self.place + "\n")
        return types.SimpleNamespace(stdout="")

    def usb_serial(self) -> str | None:
        if self.place != "nothing":
            return "G000"
        if len(self.places) > 1:
            self.places.pop(0)
        return None


@pytest.fixture
def legs(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
    ran: list[dict[str, object]] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="probed", target=support, value=lambda: "none")
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="into_recovery", target=support, value=lambda **_: True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="usb_leg", target=support, value=lambda **_: 4)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="emmc_leg", target=support, value=lambda **o: ran.append(o)
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="mmc_said", target=support, value=lambda **_: ["mmc"])
    return ran


@pytest.fixture
def lines() -> list[tuple[str, object]]:
    return []


@pytest.fixture
def shown(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> list[str]:
    texts: list[str] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="show", target=support, value=lambda **said: texts.append(said["text"])
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="run",
        target=support,
        value=lambda **_: types.SimpleNamespace(stdout="Android Debug Bridge 1.0.41\n"),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="probed", target=support, value=lambda: "none")
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="dot_details", target=support, value=lambda **_: ["dot"])
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="mmc_said", target=support, value=lambda **_: ["mmc"])
    return texts


def dot(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, places: list[str]) -> Dot:
    fake = Dot(places=places)
    for name in ("adb_shell", "getvar", "in_fastboot", "run", "usb_serial"):
        monkeypatch.setattr(name=name, target=support, value=getattr(fake, name))
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="sleep", target=support.time, value=lambda _: None)
    return fake


def recorder(*, lines: list[tuple[str, object]]) -> Callable[[str, object], None]:
    return lambda label, value: lines.append((label, value))


def test_a_report_answers_nothing_for_a_silent_read(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, shown: list[str]
) -> None:
    del adb
    said: list[str] = []

    def details(*, asked: Callable[..., str]) -> list[str]:
        said.append(asked(command="getprop ro.serialno"))
        return []

    monkeypatch.setattr(name="into_recovery", target=support, value=lambda **_: True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="dot_details", target=support, value=details)
    support.report()
    assert said == ["<nothing>"]
    assert shown


def test_a_report_names_the_firebreak_version(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, shown: list[str]
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="into_recovery", target=support, value=lambda **_: False)
    support.report()
    assert support.labelled(label="firebreak version", value=support.__version__) in (
        shown
    )
    assert shown[-1] == "\nThe report stops with what is above."


@pytest.mark.parametrize("built", [False, True])
def test_a_report_reads_the_dot_s_recovery(
    *,
    adb: FakeAdb,
    built: bool,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    shown: list[str],
) -> None:
    if built:
        held = support.CACHE / f"system-{support.FIREOS.sha256[:12]}" / "md5"
        held.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
        held.write_text("abc 2 1\n")
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="into_recovery", target=support, value=lambda **_: True)
    adb.answers["ls -l"] = [LISTING]
    adb.answers["cat /proc/partitions"] = [PARTITIONS]
    support.report()
    text = "\n".join(shown)
    assert "dot" in shown
    assert "mmc" in shown
    if built:
        assert "8192 bytes, md5 abc" in text
    else:
        assert "not built yet" in text
    assert shown[-1] == "\n" + support.LEFT_IN_RECOVERY


def test_into_recovery_gives_up_after_its_wait(
    *, lines: list[tuple[str, object]], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    dot(monkeypatch=monkeypatch, places=["nothing"])
    clock = iter(range(0, 10 * support.WAIT, support.WAIT // 2))
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="monotonic", target=support.time, value=lambda: next(clock)
    )
    assert support.into_recovery(line=recorder(lines=lines)) is False


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "places",
    [
        ["recovery"],
        ["fastboot", "recovery"],
        ["device", "recovery"],
        ["recovery", "recovery"],
        ["nothing", "nothing", "fastboot", "recovery"],
    ],
)
def test_into_recovery_reaches_twrp_from_where_the_dot_is(
    *,
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    lines: list[tuple[str, object]],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    places: list[str],
) -> None:
    start = places[0]
    fake = dot(monkeypatch=monkeypatch, places=places)
    assert support.into_recovery(line=recorder(lines=lines)) is True
    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    if start == "fastboot":
        assert ("fastboot product", "BISCUIT") in lines
        assert ["fastboot", "oem", "reboot-recovery"] in fake.ran
    if start == "device":
        assert ["adb", "reboot", "recovery"] in fake.ran
    if start == "nothing":
        assert out.count("comes from the Dot's recovery") == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("places", "change", "said"),
    [
        (["fastboot"], ("variables", "unlock_status", ""), "did not say whether"),
        (["fastboot"], ("variables", "unlock_status", "false"), "is locked"),
        (["recovery"], ("said", "getprop ro.twrp.version", ""), "not TWRP"),
        (["device"], ("said", "id; su -c id", "uid=2000(shell)"), "without root"),
        (["fastboot", "lost"], ("said", "", ""), "within 3 minutes"),
    ],
)
def test_into_recovery_says_why_it_stops(  # ruff: ignore[too-many-arguments]
    *,
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    change: tuple[str, str, str],
    lines: list[tuple[str, object]],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    places: list[str],
    said: str,
) -> None:
    fake = dot(monkeypatch=monkeypatch, places=places)
    table, key, value = change
    getattr(fake, table)[key] = value
    assert support.into_recovery(line=recorder(lines=lines)) is False
    assert said in " ".join(capsys.readouterr().out.split())


def test_the_write_test_cleans_up_after_a_failed_leg(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, legs: list[dict[str, object]], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    del legs

    def fail(**_: object) -> int:
        message = "leg"
        raise RuntimeError(message)

    monkeypatch.setattr(name="usb_leg", target=support, value=fail)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="leg"):
        support.write_test()
    assert adb.commands(holding="rm -f")


def test_the_write_test_needs_a_recovery(
    *,
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    legs: list[dict[str, object]],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="into_recovery", target=support, value=lambda **_: False)
    support.write_test()
    assert legs == []
    assert "needs a recovery" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_the_write_test_runs_both_legs_and_cleans_up(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str], legs: list[dict[str, object]]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["toybox dd --help"] = ["yes"]
    adb.answers["ls -l"] = [LISTING]
    adb.answers["cat /proc/partitions"] = [PARTITIONS]
    support.write_test()
    assert legs[0]["node"] == "/dev/block/mmcblk0p3"
    assert legs[0]["blocks"] == 64
    assert legs[0]["chunk"] == 4
    assert support.SESSION.dd == "toybox dd"
    assert adb.commands(holding="rm -f") == [f"rm -f {support.USB_TEST}"]
    assert capsys.readouterr().out.endswith(support.LEFT_IN_RECOVERY + "\n")


def test_the_write_test_skips_the_emmc_leg_without_whole_lists(
    *,
    adb: FakeAdb,
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    legs: list[dict[str, object]],
) -> None:
    adb.answers["ls -l"] = [subprocess.TimeoutExpired(cmd="adb", timeout=60)]
    support.write_test()
    assert legs == []
    assert "did not arrive whole" in capsys.readouterr().out
```

`tests/test_targets.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

from firebreak.devices import State
from firebreak.plugin import FireOs5, FireOs6
from firebreak.unlocks.amonet_biscuit_v1_1_0 import AMONET_BISCUIT_V1_1_0
from firebreak.unlocks.amonet_biscuit_v1_1_0_bboe import AMONET_BISCUIT_V1_1_0_BBOE
from firebreak.unlocks.amonet_biscuit_v2_0_0 import AMONET_BISCUIT_V2_0_0
from firebreak.unlocks.targets import FIREOS, TARGETS


def test_each_target_names_its_goal_its_install_and_its_unlock() -> None:
    assert {
        name: (target.goal, target.installs, target.unlock)
        for name, target in TARGETS.items()
    } == {
        "amonet-biscuit-v1.1.0": (
            State.AMONET_V1_1_0_BOOTED,
            FireOs5(package=FIREOS),
            AMONET_BISCUIT_V1_1_0,
        ),
        "amonet-biscuit-v1.1.0-bboe": (
            State.AMONET_V1_1_0_BBOE_BOOTED,
            FireOs5(package=FIREOS),
            AMONET_BISCUIT_V1_1_0_BBOE,
        ),
        "amonet-biscuit-v2.0.0": (
            State.AMONET_V2_0_0_BOOTED,
            FireOs6(build="8146"),
            AMONET_BISCUIT_V2_0_0,
        ),
        "stock": (State.STOCK_BOOTED, FireOs6(build=None), None),
    }
    assert all(target.name == name for name, target in TARGETS.items())
```

`tests/test_twrp.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

import gzip
import hashlib
import os
import subprocess
import time
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

import pytest

from firebreak import twrp
from firebreak.ui import FatalError, explained

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    import pathlib

    from conftest import FakeAdb

TABLE = """\
Number  Start (sector)    End (sector)  Size       Code  Name
   1            1024            2047   512.0 KiB   0700  boot_a
  12           65536          131071   32.0 MiB    8300  userdata
table-ok"""


@pytest.fixture
def carried() -> None:
    twrp.SESSION.carries = True


@pytest.fixture
def erased() -> pathlib.Path:
    twrp.ERASED.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
    twrp.ERASED.touch()
    twrp.SESSION.boot0_marker = twrp.ERASED
    return twrp.ERASED


@pytest.fixture
def image(*, tmp_path: pathlib.Path) -> pathlib.Path:
    path = tmp_path / "image.img"
    path.write_bytes(b"I" * 8192)
    return path


@pytest.fixture
def slices(*, tmp_path: pathlib.Path) -> list[pathlib.Path]:
    paths = [tmp_path / "system.00.gz", tmp_path / "system.01.gz"]
    for index, path in enumerate(paths):
        path.write_bytes(gzip.compress(data=bytes([index]) * 64))
    return paths


def md5(*, data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.md5(data, usedforsecurity=False).hexdigest() + "  -"


def test_a_block_device_check_names_each_wrong_node(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.answers["nodes-ok"] = [f"{twrp.DISK}p3=no nodes-ok"]
    wrong = twrp.check_nodes(want={f"{twrp.DISK}p3": 512, f"{twrp.DISK}p5": 1024})
    assert wrong == f"{twrp.DISK}p3 reads no, not 512 bytes"


def test_a_block_device_check_naming_a_node_not_asked_about_is_read_again(
    *, adb: FakeAdb
) -> None:
    adb.answers["nodes-ok"] = [f"{twrp.DISK}p9=no nodes-ok", " nodes-ok"]
    assert twrp.check_nodes(want={f"{twrp.DISK}p3": 512}) == ""
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="wait-for-recovery")) == 1


def test_a_block_device_check_that_never_answers_stops_it(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.answers["nodes-ok"] = ["b= nodes-"]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError) as stopped:
        twrp.check_nodes(want={f"{twrp.DISK}p3": 512})
    assert "which of its partitions are block devices: 'b= nodes-'" in " ".join(
        explained(error=stopped.value).split()
    )
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="nodes-ok")) == twrp.PUSH_TRIES
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="wait-for-recovery")) == twrp.PUSH_TRIES - 1


def test_a_block_device_check_with_every_node_right_says_nothing(
    *, adb: FakeAdb
) -> None:
    adb.answers["nodes-ok"] = [" nodes-ok"]
    assert twrp.check_nodes(want={f"{twrp.DISK}p3": 512}) == ""
    (asked,) = adb.commands(holding="nodes-ok")
    assert f"for p in {twrp.DISK}p3:512;" in asked


def test_a_cleared_boot0_goes_on(*, adb: FakeAdb, erased: pathlib.Path) -> None:
    adb.answers["force_ro"] = ["4096 0"]
    twrp.clear_boot0()
    assert erased.exists()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    argnames=("answer", "started", "said"),
    argvalues=[
        ("left:/cache ", False, "still mounted: /cache; nothing was written"),
        ("left:/cache ", True, "still mounted: /cache; do not reboot"),
        ("umount: busy", False, "did not answer what is mounted"),
    ],
    ids=["mounted", "mounted-mid-restore", "no-answer"],
)
def test_a_mount_left_behind_stops_it(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, answer: str, said: str, started: bool
) -> None:
    adb.answers["left:"] = [answer]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError) as stopped:
        twrp.unmount(started=started)
    assert said in " ".join(explained(error=stopped.value).split())


def test_a_partition_table_cut_short_is_read_again(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.answers["sgdisk --print"] = [TABLE[:40], TABLE]
    assert twrp.partition_table() == TABLE.removesuffix("\ntable-ok")
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="wait-for-recovery")) == 1


def test_a_partition_table_is_read_by_name(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.answers["sgdisk --print"] = [TABLE]
    assert twrp.partitions() == {
        "boot_a": (1, 1024, 2047),
        "userdata": (12, 65536, 131071),
    }


def test_a_partition_table_that_never_arrives_stops_it(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.answers["sgdisk --print"] = ["Problem opening /dev/block/mmcblk0"]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="did not print"):
        twrp.partition_table()
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="sgdisk")) == twrp.PUSH_TRIES


def test_a_partition_table_without_a_userdata_row_stops_it(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.answers["sgdisk --print"] = ["Disk userdata\ntable-ok"]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="no userdata"):
        twrp.partitions()


def test_a_partition_table_without_userdata_is_read_again(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.answers["sgdisk --print"] = ["Number  Start\ntable-ok", TABLE]
    assert twrp.partition_table() == TABLE.removesuffix("\ntable-ok")
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="wait-for-recovery")) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    argnames=("options", "arguments", "estimate"),
    argvalues=[
        ({}, ["adb", "reboot", "recovery"], "40 s"),
        ({"estimate": "4 min", "into": ""}, ["adb", "reboot"], "4 min"),
    ],
    ids=["recovery", "system"],
)
def test_a_reboot_waits_where_it_was_sent(
    *,
    arguments: list[str],
    estimate: str,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    options: dict[str, str],
) -> None:
    ran: list[dict[str, object]] = []
    begun: list[dict[str, object]] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="run", target=twrp, value=lambda **o: ran.append(o))
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="begin", target=twrp.PROGRESS, value=lambda **o: begun.append(o)
    )
    twrp.reboot(label="waiting", **options)
    assert ran == [{"arguments": arguments, "check": True, "timeout": 60}]
    assert begun == [{"estimate": estimate, "label": "waiting"}]


def test_a_sector_read_returns_whole_sectors(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.raw = b"S" * 1024
    assert twrp.read_sectors(count=2, start=34) == b"S" * 1024
    (asked,) = adb.commands(holding="if=")
    assert f"dd if={twrp.DISK} bs=512 skip=34 count=2" in asked


def test_a_short_sector_read_stops_it(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.raw = b"S" * 1000
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="read 1000 bytes"):
        twrp.read_sectors(count=2, start=0)


def test_a_slice_marker_that_is_not_the_last_line_is_a_failure(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["gunzip"] = [twrp.SLICE_OK + "\ndd: write error"]
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=slices[0].read_bytes())]
    assert twrp.sliced_system(slices=slices, system="/s") == (
        twrp.SLICE_OK + "\ndd: write error"
    )


def test_a_slice_that_arrived_but_does_not_unpack_is_discarded(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    twrp.SESSION.carries = False
    adb.answers["echo ready"] = ["ready"]
    adb.answers["gunzip"] = [twrp.GUNZIP_FAILED]
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=slices[0].read_bytes())]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="after 3 tries") as stopped:
        twrp.write_system(blocks=2, slices=slices, system="/s", want="new")
    said = " ".join(explained(error=stopped.value).split())
    assert "system.00.gz arrived intact but did not unpack" in said
    assert "next run rebuilds it" in said
    assert not slices[0].exists()
    assert slices[1].exists()
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="gunzip")) == 1
    assert adb.commands(holding="wait-for-recovery") == []


def test_a_slice_that_exits_badly_says_what_it_printed(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["gunzip"] = [(9, "dd: /s: Read-only file system\n")]
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=slices[0].read_bytes())]
    assert (
        twrp.sliced_system(slices=slices, system="/s")
        == "dd: /s: Read-only file system"
    )


def test_a_slice_the_dot_says_nothing_about_is_a_failure(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["gunzip"] = [""]
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=slices[0].read_bytes())]
    assert (
        twrp.sliced_system(slices=slices, system="/s")
        == "the Dot said nothing about system.00.gz"
    )


def test_a_staged_write_lands_through_dd(*, adb: FakeAdb, image: pathlib.Path) -> None:
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=b"old"), md5(data=image.read_bytes())]
    adb.answers["of=" + twrp.DISK] = ["written"]
    adb.answers["flushed"] = ["flushed"]
    twrp.write(estimate="5 s", label="table", path=image, sector=8)
    (written,) = adb.commands(holding="of=" + twrp.DISK)
    assert written.startswith(
        f"dd if={twrp.DOT_TEMPORARY_DIRECTORY / image.name} of={twrp.DISK} bs=4096"
        " seek=1 && rm -f"
    )


def test_a_staged_write_that_dd_does_not_finish_stops_it(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, image: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=b"old")]
    adb.answers["of=" + twrp.DISK] = ["dd: write error"]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="table failed"):
        twrp.write(estimate="5 s", label="table", path=image, sector=1)
    assert "bs=512 seek=1" in adb.commands(holding="of=" + twrp.DISK)[0]


def test_a_staged_write_that_does_not_reach_the_dot_stops_it(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, image: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=b"old")]
    adb.answers[str(twrp.DOT_TEMPORARY_DIRECTORY / image.name)] = [(1, "no space")]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="did not reach the Dot"):
        twrp.write(estimate="5 s", label="table", path=image, sector=8)


def test_a_stdin_probe_that_does_not_finish_takes_the_pieces_and_says_so(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["cat >"] = [subprocess.TimeoutExpired(cmd=["adb"], timeout=60)]
    assert not twrp.stdin_carries()
    assert "did not carry a 1 KiB probe" in capsys.readouterr().out
    assert not list(twrp.CACHE.glob("stdin-probe*"))


def test_a_stdin_probe_that_fails_takes_the_pieces_and_says_so(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["cat >"] = [(1, "error: closed")]
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [lambda: md5(data=adb.sent[-1])]
    assert not twrp.stdin_carries()
    assert "did not carry a 1 KiB probe" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_a_stdin_that_carries_the_probe_streams(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [lambda: md5(data=adb.sent[-1])]
    assert twrp.stdin_carries()
    assert twrp.stdin_carries()
    assert len(adb.sent) == 1
    assert adb.sent[0][:16] == b"\x1a" * 16
    assert len(adb.sent[0]) == twrp.STDIN_PROBE_BYTES
    assert not list(twrp.CACHE.glob("stdin-probe*"))


def test_a_stdin_that_cuts_the_probe_takes_the_pieces_quietly(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=b"")]
    assert not twrp.stdin_carries()
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == ""


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("carried")
def test_a_stream_gunzip_cannot_read_is_named(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["echo ready"] = ["ready"]
    adb.answers["gunzip"] = [twrp.GUNZIP_FAILED]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="gunzip could not read"):
        twrp.write_system(blocks=2, slices=slices, system="/s", want="new")
    assert slices[0].exists()


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("carried")
def test_a_stream_marker_that_is_not_the_last_line_is_a_failure(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["gunzip"] = [twrp.STREAM_OK + "\nnoise"]
    assert twrp.streamed_system(slices=slices, system="/s") == (
        twrp.STREAM_OK + "\nnoise"
    )


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("carried")
def test_a_stream_that_fails_on_a_slice_bad_here_discards_it(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    slices[1].write_bytes(slices[1].read_bytes()[:-8] + b"\0" * 8)
    adb.answers["echo ready"] = ["ready"]
    adb.answers["gunzip"] = [twrp.GUNZIP_FAILED]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="after 3 tries") as stopped:
        twrp.write_system(blocks=2, slices=slices, system="/s", want="new")
    said = " ".join(explained(error=stopped.value).split())
    assert "system.01.gz did not unpack on this computer either" in said
    assert slices[0].exists()
    assert not slices[1].exists()
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="gunzip")) == 1


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("carried")
def test_a_stream_the_dot_says_nothing_about_is_a_failure(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["echo ready"] = ["ready"]
    adb.answers["gunzip"] = [""]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="the Dot said nothing"):
        twrp.write_system(blocks=2, slices=slices, system="/s", want="new")


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("carried")
def test_a_system_image_that_never_finishes_keeps_the_cache(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["echo ready"] = ["ready"]
    adb.answers["gunzip"] = [subprocess.TimeoutExpired(cmd=["adb"], timeout=600)]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="did not finish"):
        twrp.write_system(blocks=2, slices=slices, system="/s", want="new")
    assert slices[0].exists()


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("carried")
def test_a_system_image_that_never_verifies_is_discarded(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["echo ready"] = ["ready"]
    adb.answers["gunzip"] = [twrp.STREAM_OK]
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=b"old")]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="cached image was discarded"):
        twrp.write_system(blocks=2, slices=slices, system="/s", want="new")
    assert not slices[0].exists()


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("carried")
def test_a_system_partition_left_mounted_stops_it(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    adb.answers["echo ready"] = ["umount: /system_root: busy"]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="stayed mounted"):
        twrp.write_system(blocks=2, slices=slices, system="/s", want="new")
    assert adb.sent == []


def test_a_write_already_in_place_is_skipped(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, image: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=image.read_bytes())]
    twrp.write(estimate="5 s", label="table", number=7, path=image, sector=8)
    assert [words for words in adb.asked if words[1] == "push"] == []
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="md5sum")) == 1


def test_a_write_by_partition_checks_where_it_starts(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, image: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=b"old")]
    adb.answers["/start"] = ["9"]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="starts at 9, not 8"):
        twrp.write(estimate="5 s", label="boot", number=7, path=image, sector=8)
    assert [words for words in adb.asked if words[1] == "push"] == []


def test_a_write_by_partition_is_pushed_to_its_node(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, image: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=b"old"), md5(data=image.read_bytes())]
    adb.answers["/start"] = ["8"]
    adb.answers["flushed"] = ["flushed"]
    twrp.write(estimate="5 s", label="boot", number=7, path=image, sector=8)
    assert ["adb", "push", str(image), f"{twrp.DISK}p7"] in adb.asked


def test_a_write_by_partition_that_push_refuses_stops_it(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, image: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=b"old")]
    adb.answers["/start"] = ["8"]
    adb.answers[f"{twrp.DISK}p7"] = [(1, "remote write failed")]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="remote write failed"):
        twrp.write(estimate="5 s", label="boot", number=7, path=image, sector=8)


def test_a_write_that_does_not_flush_stops_it(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, image: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=b"old")]
    adb.answers["of=" + twrp.DISK] = ["written"]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="could not be flushed"):
        twrp.write(estimate="5 s", label="table", path=image, sector=8)


def test_a_write_that_does_not_verify_stops_it(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, erased: pathlib.Path, image: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    del erased
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=b"old")]
    adb.answers["of=" + twrp.DISK] = ["written"]
    adb.answers["flushed"] = ["flushed"]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="did not verify") as stopped:
        twrp.write(estimate="5 s", label="table", path=image, sector=8)
    assert "boot0 may hold no preloader" in " ".join(
        explained(error=stopped.value).split()
    )


def test_a_write_with_a_matching_head_still_checks_the_whole(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, image: pathlib.Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="HEAD_CHECK", target=twrp, value=4096)
    head = md5(data=image.read_bytes()[:4096])
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [head, md5(data=b"old"), md5(data=image.read_bytes())]
    adb.answers["of=" + twrp.DISK] = ["written"]
    adb.answers["flushed"] = ["flushed"]
    twrp.write(estimate="5 s", label="table", path=image, sector=8)
    reads = adb.commands(holding="md5sum")
    assert "count=1 " in reads[0]
    assert "count=2 " in reads[1]
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="of=" + twrp.DISK)) == 1


def test_an_uncleared_boot0_header_stops_before_the_bootrom_fallback(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, erased: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    adb.answers["force_ro"] = ["4096 12"]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="did not clear") as stopped:
        twrp.clear_boot0()
    assert not erased.exists()
    said = " ".join(explained(error=stopped.value).split())
    assert "nothing else was written" in said
    assert "boot0 may hold no preloader" not in said


def test_an_unread_boot0_says_the_dot_cannot_start(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, erased: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    del erased
    adb.answers["force_ro"] = [""]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="did not read back") as stopped:
        twrp.clear_boot0()
    assert "boot0 may hold no preloader" in " ".join(
        explained(error=stopped.value).split()
    )


def test_nothing_mounted_goes_on(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.answers["left:"] = ["left: "]
    twrp.unmount()


@pytest.mark.skipif(condition=os.name == "nt", reason="needs a POSIX shell")
def test_the_system_commands_write_and_report_through_a_real_shell(
    *, tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    target = tmp_path / "system"
    data = bytes(range(256)) * 8192
    stream = twrp.SYSTEM_STREAM.format(dd="dd", no_truncate="", system=target)
    good = subprocess.run(
        args=["sh", "-c", stream],
        capture_output=True,
        check=False,
        input=gzip.compress(data=data[: 1 << 20]) + gzip.compress(data=data[1 << 20 :]),
    )
    assert good.stdout.decode().strip().split("\n")[-1] == twrp.STREAM_OK
    assert target.read_bytes() == data
    bad = subprocess.run(
        args=["sh", "-c", stream],
        capture_output=True,
        check=False,
        input=gzip.compress(data=data)[:1000],
    )
    assert bad.stdout.decode().strip().split("\n")[-1] == twrp.GUNZIP_FAILED
    held = tmp_path / "slice.gz"
    held.write_bytes(gzip.compress(data=b"S" * (1 << 20)))
    piece = twrp.SYSTEM_SLICE.format(
        dd="dd", held=held, no_truncate="", seek=1, system=target
    )
    sliced = subprocess.run(args=["sh", "-c", piece], capture_output=True, check=False)
    assert sliced.stdout.decode().strip().split("\n")[-1] == twrp.SLICE_OK
    assert target.read_bytes()[1 << 20 : 2 << 20] == b"S" * (1 << 20)
    assert not held.exists()
    unwritable = twrp.SYSTEM_STREAM.format(
        dd="dd", no_truncate="", system=tmp_path / "missing" / "system"
    )
    failed = subprocess.run(
        args=["sh", "-c", unwritable],
        capture_output=True,
        check=False,
        input=gzip.compress(data=b"x"),
    )
    assert failed.returncode == 9


def test_the_system_image_goes_in_slices_where_stdin_cuts_it(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    twrp.SESSION.carries = False
    twrp.SESSION.dd = "toybox dd"
    system = "/dev/block/platform/mtk-msdc.0/by-name/system_a"
    adb.answers["echo ready"] = ["ready"]
    adb.answers["gunzip"] = [twrp.SLICE_OK]
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [
        md5(data=slices[0].read_bytes()),
        md5(data=slices[1].read_bytes()),
        md5(data=b"new"),
    ]
    twrp.write_system(
        blocks=2, slices=slices, system=system, want=md5(data=b"new")[:32]
    )
    written = adb.commands(holding="gunzip")
    assert [command.split(" seek=")[1].split(" ")[0] for command in written] == [
        "0",
        "128",
    ]
    assert all("conv=notrunc" in command for command in written)
    assert adb.sent == []


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("carried")
def test_the_system_image_is_streamed_until_it_verifies(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, slices: list[pathlib.Path]
) -> None:
    system = "/dev/block/platform/mtk-msdc.0/by-name/system_a"
    adb.answers["echo ready"] = ["ready"]
    adb.answers["gunzip"] = [(1, "error: closed"), twrp.STREAM_OK]
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=b"old"), md5(data=b"new")]
    twrp.write_system(
        blocks=2, slices=slices, system=system, want=md5(data=b"new")[:32]
    )
    assert adb.sent == [b"".join(path.read_bytes() for path in slices)] * 3
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="wait-for-recovery")) == 2
    assert f"dd if={system} bs=4096 count=2" in adb.commands(holding="md5sum")[0]


def test_toybox_writes_a_staged_image_without_truncating(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, image: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    twrp.SESSION.dd = "toybox dd"
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [md5(data=b"old"), md5(data=image.read_bytes())]
    adb.answers["of=" + twrp.DISK] = ["written"]
    adb.answers["flushed"] = ["flushed"]
    twrp.write(estimate="5 s", label="table", path=image, sector=8)
    (written,) = adb.commands(holding="of=" + twrp.DISK)
    assert written.startswith("toybox dd if=")
    assert "bs=4096 seek=1 conv=notrunc && rm -f" in written


def test_twrp_is_ready_once_it_runs_with_mtp(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.answers["ro.twrp.version"] = ["", "3.7.0_9-0"]
    adb.answers["sys.usb.config"] = ["mtp,adb"]
    twrp.wait_for_twrp()
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="ro.twrp.version")) == 2


def test_twrp_that_does_not_finish_starting_stops_it(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    clock = iter(range(0, 1000, 20))
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="monotonic", target=time, value=lambda: next(clock))
    adb.answers["ro.twrp.version"] = ["3.7.0_9-0"]
    adb.answers["sys.usb.config"] = ["adb"]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="within 30 seconds"):
        twrp.wait_for_twrp()


def test_twrp_that_never_comes_up_stops_it(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    adb.answers["wait-for-recovery"] = [
        subprocess.TimeoutExpired(cmd=["adb"], timeout=300)
    ]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="within 5 minutes"):
        twrp.wait_for_twrp()
```

```py
from __future__ import annotations

import io
import sys
import time
import types
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

import pytest

from firebreak import ui

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    import pathlib

URL = (
    "https://ftvdb.com/echo/firmware/com.amazon.biscuit.android.os/"
    "723886117a7a4a8a543c2ec955dcd54e-13222529668-fire-os-6574-1-ns65741-8138-2026-08-31/"
)


def test_a_fatal_error_is_said_whole_until_it_is_stopped() -> None:
    error = ui.FatalError(message="word " * 30)
    assert str(error) == "ERROR: " + "word " * 30
    assert error.message == "word " * 30
    assert ui.SESSION.shown is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    argnames=("program_path", "want"),
    argvalues=[
        ("/src/firebreak/__main__.py", "Run python3 -m firebreak stock again."),
        ("/home/me/firebreak.pyz", "Run python3 firebreak.pyz stock again."),
        ("/home/me/.local/bin/firebreak", "Run firebreak stock again."),
    ],
)
def test_again(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, program_path: str, want: str
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="argv", target=sys, value=[program_path, "stock"])
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="executable", target=sys, value="/usr/bin/python3")
    assert ui.again() == want


def test_asked_for_names_the_report_as_this_run_was_started(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="argv", target=sys, value=["/home/me/firebreak.pyz"])
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="executable", target=sys, value="/usr/bin/python3")
    said = ui.asked_for()
    assert "run python3 firebreak.pyz --report and paste" in said
    assert "#support-device-unlocking in the EchoMuse Discord" in said
    assert "python3 firebreak.pyz --write-test then tells" in said


def test_clock() -> None:
    assert time.strptime(ui.clock(), "%H:%M:%S")


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    argnames=("environment", "want"),
    argvalues=[({"NO_COLOR": "1"}, False), ({"TERM": "dumb"}, False), ({}, True)],
)
def test_color(
    *, environment: dict[str, str], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, want: bool
) -> None:
    tty(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    for name, value in environment.items():
        monkeypatch.setenv(name=name, value=value)
    assert ui.color(stream=sys.stdout) is want


def test_color_keeps_color_for_an_empty_no_color(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    tty(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    monkeypatch.setenv(name="NO_COLOR", value="")
    assert ui.color(stream=sys.stdout)


def test_color_needs_a_tty(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    tty(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="isatty", target=sys.stdout, value=lambda: False)
    assert not ui.color(stream=sys.stdout)


def test_color_needs_windows_terminal(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    tty(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="os", target=ui, value=types.SimpleNamespace(environ={}, name="nt")
    )
    assert not ui.color(stream=sys.stdout)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="environ", target=ui.os, value={"WT_SESSION": "1"})
    assert ui.color(stream=sys.stdout)


def test_explained_keeps_a_url_whole() -> None:
    error = ui.FatalError(message="no download link on " + URL)
    assert ui.explained(error=error).split("\n") == [
        "ERROR: no download link on",
        URL,
    ]


def test_explained_keeps_newlines() -> None:
    error = ui.FatalError(message="one\ntwo", prefix="")
    assert ui.explained(error=error) == "one\ntwo"


def test_explained_says_nothing_of_boot0_without_its_marker(
    *, tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    error = ui.FatalError(message="stopped", prefix="")
    ui.SESSION.boot0_marker = tmp_path / "boot0-erased"
    assert ui.explained(error=error) == "stopped"
    ui.SESSION.boot0_marker = None
    (tmp_path / "boot0-erased").touch()
    assert ui.explained(error=error) == "stopped"


def test_explained_says_the_dot_cannot_start_while_boot0_is_empty(
    *, tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    ui.SESSION.boot0_marker = tmp_path / "boot0-erased"
    ui.SESSION.boot0_marker.touch()
    first, _, note = ui.explained(
        error=ui.FatalError(message="stopped", prefix="")
    ).partition("\n\n")
    assert first == "stopped"
    assert " ".join(note.split()) == ui.BOOT0_EMPTY + "."
    assert max(len(line) for line in note.split("\n")) <= 79


def test_invocation_names_uvx_for_a_uvx_run(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="argv", target=sys, value=["/c/archive-v0/x/bin/firebreak"]
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="prefix", target=sys, value="/c/uv/archive-v0/x")
    assert ui.invocation(options=["--report"]) == "uvx firebreak@latest --report"
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="prefix", target=sys, value="/home/me/.venv")
    assert ui.invocation(options=["--report"]) == "firebreak --report"


def test_mark_falls_back_to_ascii(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    assert ui.mark() == "✅"
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="stdout",
        target=sys,
        value=io.TextIOWrapper(buffer=io.BytesIO(), encoding="ascii"),
    )
    assert ui.mark() == "done"

    assert ui.paint(code=ui.ANSIColor.RED, text="x") == "\033[31mx\033[0m"

def test_progress_aligns_two_digit_counts(
    *, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]
) -> None:
    ui.PROGRESS.steps = 16
    for _ in range(10):
        ui.PROGRESS.begin(label="step")
        ui.PROGRESS.end()
    lines = capsys.readouterr().out.splitlines()
    assert lines[0].startswith("[ 1/16] step")
    assert lines[-2].startswith("[10/16] step")


def test_progress_not_a_tty(*, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    ui.PROGRESS.steps = 2
    ui.PROGRESS.begin(estimate="1 s", label="first")
    ui.PROGRESS.begin(label="second")
    ui.PROGRESS.end(skipped=True)
    ui.PROGRESS.end()
    lines = capsys.readouterr().out.splitlines()
    assert lines[0].startswith("[1/2] first")
    assert "(~1 s)" in lines[0]
    assert "✅   0s" in lines[1]
    assert lines[2].startswith("[2/2] second")
    assert "✅ skip" in lines[3]
    assert len(lines) == 4


def test_progress_note_without_a_ticker(*, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    ui.PROGRESS.note(message="plain")
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == "plain\n"


def test_progress_spins_in_ascii(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    stream = io.TextIOWrapper(buffer=io.BytesIO(), encoding="ascii", write_through=True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="stdout", target=sys, value=stream)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="isatty", target=stream, value=lambda: True)
    ui.PROGRESS.begin(label="spin")
    time.sleep(0.15)
    ui.PROGRESS.end()
    assert b"spin" in stream.buffer.getvalue()
    assert b"|" in stream.buffer.getvalue()


def test_progress_spins_on_a_tty(
    *, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    tty(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    ui.PROGRESS.begin(label="spin")
    time.sleep(0.25)
    ui.PROGRESS.note(message="careful")
    time.sleep(0.25)
    ui.PROGRESS.end()
    output = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert output.startswith("[1/?] spin")
    assert f"{ui.SPINNER[0]}    0s\r[1/?] spin" in output
    assert "careful" in output
    assert output.endswith("(0s total)\n")
    assert ui.PROGRESS.ticker is None


def test_progress_verbose(*, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    ui.ARGUMENTS.verbose = True
    ui.PROGRESS.begin(label="loud")
    ui.PROGRESS.end()
    first, second = capsys.readouterr().out.splitlines()
    assert time.strptime(first[:8], "%H:%M:%S")
    assert first[9:].startswith("[1/?] loud")
    assert time.strptime(second[:8], "%H:%M:%S")


def test_say(
    *, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    ui.say(text="plain")
    tty(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    ui.say(code=ui.ANSIColor.YELLOW, text="yellow")
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == "plain\n\n\033[33myellow\033[0m\n"


def test_say_and_warn_keep_a_url_whole(*, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    ui.say(text="see " + URL)
    ui.warn(text="see " + URL)
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == f"see\n{URL}\n\nsee\n{URL}\n"


def test_show_separates_kinds(*, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    ui.show(text="a")
    ui.show(text="b")
    ui.show(kind=ui.Kind.WARN, text="c")
    ui.show(text="d")
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == "a\nb\n\nc\n\nd\n"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    argnames=("seconds", "want"),
    argvalues=[(59, "59s"), (60, "1m 00s"), (125, "2m 05s")],
)
def test_since(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, seconds: int, want: str) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="monotonic", target=time, value=lambda: 1000.0 + seconds)
    assert ui.since(start=1000.0) == want


def test_status(
    *, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    ui.status(text="waiting")
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == "waiting\n"
    tty(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    ui.status(text="waiting")
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == "\r\033[33m" + "waiting".ljust(79) + "\033[0m"


def test_stop_halts_progress_and_fills(
    *, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    tty(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="isatty", target=sys.stderr, value=lambda: True)
    ui.PROGRESS.begin(label="step")
    with pytest.raises(expected_exception=SystemExit) as raised:
        ui.stop(error=ui.FatalError(message="word " * 30))
    text = str(raised.value)
    assert text.startswith("\033[31mERROR: word")
    assert "\n" in text
    assert ui.PROGRESS.ticker is None
    assert ui.SESSION.shown == ui.Kind.ERROR
    assert capsys.readouterr().out.endswith("\n")


def test_stop_is_plain_without_color() -> None:
    ui.SESSION.shown = ui.Kind.INFO
    with pytest.raises(expected_exception=SystemExit) as raised:
        ui.stop(error=ui.FatalError(message="one\ntwo", prefix=""))
    assert str(raised.value) == "one\ntwo"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("argv", "prefix", "said"),
    [
        (
            "/c/archive-v0/x/bin/firebreak",
            "/c/uv/archive-v0/x",
            ("run", "uvx firebreak@latest"),
        ),
        (
            "/home/me/firebreak.pyz",
            "/usr",
            (
                "download firebreak.pyz from",
                "https://github.com/bboe/firebreak/releases/latest",
            ),
        ),
        (
            "/home/me/.venv/bin/firebreak",
            "/home/me/.venv",
            ("run", "uvx firebreak@latest"),
        ),
        ("/home/me/src/firebreak/__main__.py", "/usr", ("run", "uvx firebreak@latest")),
    ],
)
def test_upgrade_offers_the_pyz_or_uvx(
    *, argv: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, prefix: str, said: tuple[str, str]
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="argv", target=sys, value=[argv])
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="prefix", target=sys, value=prefix)
    assert ui.upgrade() == said


def tty(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="isatty", target=sys.stdout, value=lambda: True)
    monkeypatch.delenv(name="NO_COLOR", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.setenv(name="TERM", value="xterm")
    monkeypatch.setenv(name="WT_SESSION", value="1")
```

`tests/test_unlock.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import subprocess
import types
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

import pytest

from firebreak import unlock
from firebreak.ui import FatalError

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    import pathlib

    from conftest import FakeAdb

LK = {"stock": b"S" * 241664, "v1.1.0": b"1" * 372368, "v2.0.0": b"2" * 359744}


def brick(
    *,
    adb: FakeAdb,
    little_kernel: str,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: pathlib.Path,
) -> None:
    adb.answers["product"] = ["product: BISCUIT"]
    adb.answers["lk_build_desc"] = [f"lk_build_desc: {little_kernel}"]
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="unpack", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: tmp_path)


def guard(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, slots: dict[str, bytes], tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> list[str]:
    read: list[str] = []
    numbers = {"lk_a": 3, "lk_b": 5}
    held = {f"{unlock.DISK}p{numbers[slot]}": data for slot, data in slots.items()}
    releases = {
        unlock.amonet_biscuit_v1_1_0.SOURCE.name: LK["v1.1.0"],
        unlock.amonet_biscuit_v2_0_0.SOURCE.name: LK["v2.0.0"],
    }

    def unpacked(*, download: object) -> pathlib.Path:
        root = tmp_path / download.name
        (root / "bin").mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
        (root / "bin" / "lk.bin").write_bytes(releases[download.name])
        return root

    def shell(*, command: str, timeout: float) -> str:
        del timeout
        node = command.split(sep=" ")[2]
        size = int(command.split(sep="bs=")[1].split(maxsplit=1, sep=" ")[0])
        read.append(node)
        data = held[node][:size]
        return f"{hashlib.md5(data, usedforsecurity=False).hexdigest()}  -"

    monkeypatch.setattr(name="adb_shell", target=unlock, value=shell)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="partitions",
        target=unlock,
        value=lambda: {slot: (numbers[slot], 0, 0) for slot in slots},
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="unpack", target=unlock, value=unpacked)
    unlock.amonet_chain()
    return read


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("answers", "message"),
    [
        ({"unlock_status": "false"}, "not in amonet's fastboot"),
        ({"lk_build_desc": "", "unlock_status": "true"}, "boot0 was not erased"),
        (
            {"lk_build_desc": unlock.LK.FIREOS5.value, "unlock_status": "true"},
            "already runs amonet v1.1.0's bootloader",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_a_downgrade_checks_the_dot_before_it_erases_boot0(
    *,
    answers: dict[str, str],
    message: str,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: pathlib.Path,
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="unpack", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="pyserial_wheel", target=unlock, value=lambda: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="getvar", target=unlock, value=lambda **o: answers.get(o["name"], "")
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="bootrom_step", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: pytest.fail("erased")
    )
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match=message):
        unlock.stage_downgrade()


def test_a_downgrade_erases_boot0_through_fastboot(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    stepped: list[dict[str, object]] = []
    answers = {"lk_build_desc": unlock.LK.FIREOS6.value, "unlock_status": "true"}
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="unpack", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="pyserial_wheel", target=unlock, value=lambda: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="getvar", target=unlock, value=lambda **o: answers[o["name"]]
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="bootrom_step", target=unlock, value=lambda **o: stepped.append(o)
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="stage_amonet_v1_1_0_recovery", target=unlock, value=lambda: True
    )
    assert unlock.stage_downgrade() is True
    assert stepped[0]["erase"] is unlock.erase_by_fastboot
    assert stepped[0]["payload"] == tmp_path / "brom-payload" / "build" / "payload.bin"


def test_a_downgrade_that_meets_a_locked_fastboot_returns_false(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    answers = {"lk_build_desc": unlock.LK.FIREOS6.value, "unlock_status": "true"}
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="unpack", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="pyserial_wheel", target=unlock, value=lambda: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="getvar", target=unlock, value=lambda **o: answers[o["name"]]
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="bootrom_step", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="amonet_v2_0_0_payload", target=unlock, value=lambda: tmp_path
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="stage_amonet_v1_1_0_recovery", target=unlock, value=lambda: False
    )
    assert unlock.stage_downgrade() is False


def test_a_fastboot_that_never_says_whether_it_is_unlocked_stops_cleanly(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    asked: list[str] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="getvar", target=unlock, value=lambda **o: asked.append(o["name"]) or ""
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="sleep", target=unlock.time, value=lambda _: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="run", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: pytest.fail("flashed")
    )
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="did not say whether it is unlocked"):
        unlock.stage_amonet_v1_1_0_recovery()
    assert asked == ["unlock_status"] * unlock.PUSH_TRIES


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("product", "little_kernel", "message"),
    [
        ("product: crown", "x", "a product other than BISCUIT"),
        ("product: BISCUIT", "", "did not report the bootloader version"),

def test_a_fastbrick_checks_the_dot_before_it_flashes(  # ruff: ignore[too-many-arguments]
    *,

    brick(

def test_an_lk_read_answered_after_a_reconnect_is_used(
    *, adb: FakeAdb, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    (tmp_path / "bin").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "bin" / "lk.bin").write_bytes(LK["v2.0.0"])
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="unpack", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="reconnect", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: None)
    want = hashlib.md5(LK["v2.0.0"], usedforsecurity=False).hexdigest()
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = ["", f"{want}  -"]
    assert unlock.amonet_lk(node=f"{unlock.DISK}p3") is True


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("said", "message"),
    [
        ("", "did not answer with an md5 of its first"),
        ("1234  -", "did not answer with an md5 of its first"),
    ],
)
def test_an_lk_read_that_never_answers_stops_before_anything_is_written(
    *,
    adb: FakeAdb,
    message: str,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    said: str,
    tmp_path: pathlib.Path,
) -> None:
    (tmp_path / "bin").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "bin" / "lk.bin").write_bytes(LK["v2.0.0"])
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="unpack", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: tmp_path)
    reconnected: list[str] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="reconnect", target=unlock, value=lambda **o: reconnected.append(o)
    )
    adb.answers["md5sum"] = [said]
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match=message):
        unlock.amonet_lk(node=f"{unlock.DISK}p3")
    assert len(reconnected) == unlock.PUSH_TRIES - 1
    assert len(adb.commands(holding="md5sum")) == unlock.PUSH_TRIES


def test_an_unlocked_fastboot_takes_v1_1_0_to_its_recovery(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    ran: list[list[object]] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="getvar", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: "true")
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="unpack", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="fetch", target=unlock, value=lambda **_: tmp_path / "twrp"
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="run",
        target=unlock,
        value=lambda **options: ran.append(options["arguments"]),
    )
    assert unlock.stage_amonet_v1_1_0_recovery() is True
    assert [words[1:4] for words in ran] == [
        ["-S", "256M", "flash"],
        ["-S", "256M", "flash"],
        ["oem", "reboot-recovery"],
    ]


def test_erasing_boot0_reboots_the_dot(*, adb: FakeAdb) -> None:
    assert unlock.erase_by_fastboot() == ""
    assert [words[1:] for words in adb.asked] == [["erase", "boot0"], ["reboot"]]


def test_erasing_boot0_that_fails_leaves_no_marker(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    unlock.ERASED.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
    unlock.ERASED.touch()
    answers = iter([1, 0, 1])
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="run",
        target=unlock,
        value=lambda **_: types.SimpleNamespace(returncode=next(answers), stdout="no"),
    )
    assert unlock.erase_by_fastboot().startswith("fastboot erase boot0 failed")
    assert not unlock.ERASED.exists()
    unlock.ERASED.touch()
    assert unlock.erase_by_fastboot().startswith("fastboot reboot failed")
    assert unlock.ERASED.exists()

@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    argnames=("slots", "nodes"),
    argvalues=[
        ({"lk_a": LK["v2.0.0"], "lk_b": LK["stock"]}, ["p3"]),
        ({"lk_a": LK["v1.1.0"], "lk_b": LK["stock"]}, ["p3", "p3"]),
        (
            {"lk_a": LK["v1.1.0"][:4096] + LK["v2.0.0"][4096:], "lk_b": LK["v2.0.0"]},
            ["p3", "p3", "p5"],
        ),
        (
            {"lk_a": LK["v2.0.0"][:4096] + LK["stock"][4096:], "lk_b": LK["v1.1.0"]},
            ["p3", "p3", "p5", "p5"],
        ),
    ],
    ids=["v2.0.0", "v1.1.0", "lk_a-torn", "lk_a-torn-over-stock"],
)
def test_the_guard_passes_an_amonet_lk_in_either_slot(
    *,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    nodes: list[str],
    slots: dict[str, bytes],
    tmp_path: pathlib.Path,
) -> None:
    read = guard(monkeypatch=monkeypatch, slots=slots, tmp_path=tmp_path)
    assert [node[len(unlock.DISK) :] for node in read] == nodes

    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> None:

        guard(
            monkeypatch=monkeypatch,
            slots={"lk_a": LK["stock"], "lk_b": LK["stock"]},
            tmp_path=tmp_path,
        )
```

`tests/test_updates.py`:

```py
from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import hashlib
import http.client
import io
import threading
import urllib.request
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

import pytest

from firebreak import updates
from firebreak.ui import FatalError

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    import pathlib

BODY = b"OTA" * 1000
LINK = "https://cdn.example/update-kindle-biscuit.bin"


class Response(io.BytesIO):
    def __init__(self, *, body: bytes, length: str | None = None) -> None:
        super().__init__(initial_bytes=body)
        self.headers = http.client.HTTPMessage()
        if length is not None:
            self.headers["Content-Length"] = length


@pytest.fixture
def build(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> updates.Build:
    details = dataclasses.replace(
        updates.BUILDS["8146"], sha256=hashlib.sha256(BODY).hexdigest()
    )
    monkeypatch.setitem(updates.BUILDS, "8146", details)
    return details


def served(
    *,
    body: bytes = BODY,
    length: str | None = None,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    page: bytes,
) -> list[str]:
    asked: list[str] = []

    def urlopen(*, timeout: float, url: str | urllib.request.Request) -> Response:
        del timeout
        if isinstance(url, urllib.request.Request):
            asked.append(url.full_url)
            return Response(body=page)
        asked.append(url)
        return Response(body=body, length=length)

    monkeypatch.setattr(name="urlopen", target=urllib.request, value=urlopen)
    return asked


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("build")
def test_a_build_checked_off_the_main_thread_says_nothing(
    *, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    served(monkeypatch=monkeypatch, page=LINK.encode())
    worker = threading.Thread(target=lambda: updates.download(build="8146"))
    worker.start()
    worker.join()
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == ""
    assert updates.SESSION.verified


def test_a_build_is_fetched_from_its_ftvdb_page(
    *,
    build: updates.Build,
    capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str],
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    asked = served(monkeypatch=monkeypatch, page=f'<a href="{LINK}">'.encode())
    update = updates.download(build="8146")
    assert update.read_bytes() == BODY
    assert update.name == (
        f"update-kindle-biscuit_puffin-{build.ns}_user_8146_00{build.number}.bin"
    )
    assert asked[0].startswith(updates.FTVDB + build.md5)
    assert asked[0].endswith(f"-8146-{build.date}/")
    assert asked[1] == LINK
    assert "OTA 8146 verified" in capsys.readouterr().out
    assert not (update.parent / (update.name + ".part")).exists()
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="urlopen", target=urllib.request, value=lambda **_: pytest.fail("fetched")
    )
    hashed = updates.digest
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        name="digest", target=updates, value=lambda **_: pytest.fail("hashed")
    )
    assert updates.download(build="8146") == update
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="digest", target=updates, value=hashed)
    updates.SESSION.verified.clear()
    assert updates.download(build="8146") == update


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("build")
def test_a_cached_build_with_the_wrong_hash_is_fetched_again(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    asked = served(monkeypatch=monkeypatch, page=LINK.encode())
    first = updates.download(build="8146")
    first.write_bytes(b"damaged")
    updates.SESSION.verified.clear()
    assert updates.download(build="8146").read_bytes() == BODY
    assert len(asked) == 4


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("build")
def test_a_download_cut_short_stops_the_run(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    served(length="999999", monkeypatch=monkeypatch, page=LINK.encode())
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="stopped after 3000 of 999999 bytes"):
        updates.download(build="8146")


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("build")
def test_a_download_with_the_wrong_hash_stops_the_run(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    served(body=b"wrong", monkeypatch=monkeypatch, page=LINK.encode())
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match="does not hash to"):
        updates.download(build="8146")
    assert not updates.SESSION.verified


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("build")
def test_a_failed_download_stops_the_run(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    def refuse(**_: object) -> Response:
        raise http.client.RemoteDisconnected

    monkeypatch.setattr(name="urlopen", target=urllib.request, value=refuse)
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match=r"the download failed: RemoteDisconnected\("):
        updates.download(build="8146")


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("build")
def test_a_page_with_no_link_stops_the_run(*, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    served(monkeypatch=monkeypatch, page=b"<html>gone</html>")
    with pytest.raises(FatalError, match=r"no download link on https://ftvdb\.com/"):
        updates.download(build="8146")


def test_every_build_has_whole_pins() -> None:
    for name, details in updates.BUILDS.items():
        assert name.isdigit()
        assert len(details.md5) == 32
        assert len(details.sha256) == 64
        assert details.ns.startswith("NS")


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("build")
def test_the_cache_is_made_where_it_is_missing(
    *, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: pathlib.Path
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(name="CACHE", target=updates, value=tmp_path / "a" / "b")
    served(monkeypatch=monkeypatch, page=LINK.encode())
    assert updates.download(build="8146").parent == tmp_path / "a" / "b"
```
